# xbot

> Go AI Agent framework with message bus + plugin architecture. Supports Feishu/QQ/CLI/Web channels, tool calling, pluggable memory, skills, subagents, MCP integration.

## Quick Reference

- Entry points: `cmd/xbot-cli/` (CLI), `cmd/runner/` (remote sandbox), `cmd/xbot/` (server)
- Build: `go build ./...` | Test: `go test ./...` | Lint: `golangci-lint run ./...`
- Config: `~/.xbot/config.json`, env var overrides
- Subscriptions: `~/.xbot/config.json` (CLI) or DB `user_llm_subscriptions` (Server) — the single source of truth for LLM config
- Pre-commit: gofmt → golangci-lint → go build → go test
- **本地 pre-commit hook（`.githooks/pre-commit`）**：对齐 CI 全部本地可执行检查（golangci-lint / go build / go test / 前端 eslint+vitest+build）。启用：`git config core.hooksPath .githooks`（每 clone 需执行一次，团队共享）。SKIP_RACE=1 跳过 race 检测（本地提速，CI 仍跑）；RUN_E2E=1 额外跑 Playwright E2E（默认跳过，耗时）。golangci-lint 须用**官方 release 二进制**（`curl -sSfL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golangci/golangci-lint/master/install.sh | sh -s -- -b $(go env GOPATH)/bin v2.10.1`）——`go install` 编译的版本内置 go1.25 会报「lower than targeted go 1.26」。
- **禁止 AI agent 自行重启 xbot server**：`kill`/`pkill` xbot 进程、`nohup` 重新启动 server 等操作会中断用户正在进行的会话。除非用户明确要求重启，否则只 build 二进制 + 前端部署，由用户自行重启。前端改动 `vite build` 后 `cp dist/*` 部署即可，无需重启 server（唯一例外：改了 Go 侧静态服务/Cache-Control 时必须重启，用 `nohup bash -c 'sleep 10 && supervisorctl restart xbot-server' &` 后台异步调度，supervisorctl 不在 PATH —— 用 nix store 完整路径 `/nix/store/5mc40v8qa34jyilh5jgsfi1sc42f77hv-python3.8-supervisor-4.2.2/bin/supervisorctl`）。
- **⚠️ 前端"修复部署了但用户说没用"先查 SW 缓存（浪费了多轮排查的真凶）**：静态服务曾用裸 `http.FileServer`（web.go）——`sw.js` 无 `Cache-Control` 响应头 → 浏览器按 RFC 9111 启发式缓存 SW 脚本（最长 24h fresh）→ SW 更新检查命中 HTTP 缓存的旧 `sw.js` → **新 SW 永不安装 → precache 永远旧 bundle → SW 拦截一切静态请求（连 index.html 都从旧 precache 出）→ 用户刷新 N 次都跑旧代码**。诊断特征：server 日志**零 assets 请求回源**（只有 `GET /`）；本地 E2E（dev 无 SW）全绿。修复三层：① `serveStaticFile`：`sw.js`→`no-store`、`index.html`→`no-cache`、`assets/*`→`immutable 1y`；② SW 改名 `sw.js`→`sw2.js`（vite `filename` + PWAUpdatePrompt 注册 URL）绕开已污染缓存的旧 URL，注册时顺带 unregister 旧 SW；③ 改 Cache-Control 是 Go 改动，**必须重启 server 才生效**。验证命令：`curl -s --noproxy '*' -D - -o /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:16000/sw2.js`（本地 curl 必须带 `--noproxy '*'`，否则 502——本地代理拦截）。
- Issue templates: `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/` — YAML forms (`*.yml`) for web UI, Markdown templates (`*.md`) for CLI/AI use with `gh issue create --template`. AI agents MUST read and fill the `.md` templates (not `.yml`) since YAML Issue Forms are web-UI-only and cannot be submitted via `gh issue create --body`.

- `docs/agent/agent.md` — agent loop, middleware, SubAgent, context management, masking, dynamic context, reminder
- `docs/agent/llm.md` — LLM clients, streaming pitfalls, retry behavior, model tiers (vanguard/balance/swift)
- `docs/agent/subscription.md` — **subscription system 完整文档**: LLMFactory cache、GetLLM/GetLLMForChat/GetLLMForModel 解析链、max_context 优先级、所有切换场景（per-session/全局/settings/启动恢复）、会话隔离规则、Invalidate 速查表、TUI↔Backend 数据同步、**UserContext 统一解析（ResolveUserContext）**、**v63 多用户删除（单 operator + admin allowlist）**
- `docs/agent/tools.md` — built-in tools: Shell, Read, Edit, Glob, Grep, Cd, Fetch, WebSearch, Cron, SubAgent, CreateChat, SendMessage, Worktree, config, tui_control, TodoWrite, context_edit, AskUser, DownloadFile, ChatHistory, ManageTools, Skill, EventTrigger, TaskManager, hooks system (agent/hooks/), sandbox types, ChannelPluginTransport (stdio channel plugin transport)
- `docs/agent/settings.md` — settings system: single registry (agent/setting_runtime.go), cli_settings.go, UpdatePerModelConfig, subscription-scoped vs user-scoped, runtime apply chain
- `docs/agent/conventions.md` — error handling, logging, testing, naming, build, **local/remote unification**
- `docs/agent/hooks.md` — hooks lifecycle events, handler types, configuration, gotchas
- `docs/agent/channel.md` — CLI (BubbleTea TUI), Feishu, Web, QQ adapters, asyncCh pattern, deterministic rendering, mouse support, settings panels
- `docs/agent/memory.md` — letta vs flat providers; **xbot provider BM25 搜索 OR 语义**（`fts5OrQuery` 召回 + bm25 排序；dedup 保持 `fts5SafeQuery` AND —— 多词查询一个词缺席不能零化结果集，"frpc vs frps" 用户报告根因）
- `docs/agent/plugin.md` — plugin system architecture, runtimes, integration, RPC bridge
- `docs/agent/worktree.md` — git worktree-based multi-agent workspace isolation, WorktreeRegistry, AutoDetectAndInit, peer discovery, path security
- `docs/agent/web-message-store.md` — **Web 消息渲染架构（方案 A）**: MessageStore 单一消息状态机（Map<turnID, TurnSlot>，每 turn 1 user + 1 assistant，live 是 assistant 的未完成态）。取代 "两套数据 + buildMessageRows 启发式去重" —— 唯一性由结构保证，渲染层零去重，从根上消除 "turn 消失/重复" 整类 bug（exactDup 跨 turn 迭代号匹配等）。buildMessageRows/liveMessage prop/dedupMessages 已删除
- `docs/agent/web-consistency-design.md` — **Web 消息一致性设计（Raft 模型）**: Log（eventStream ring + DB）→ Snapshot（get_history/active_progress）→ State Machine（ProgressStore/MessageStore）映射；两个独立 seq 序列（SSE envelope per-route vs ProgressEvent.Seq per-Run，混用即 bug）；已保证的一致性（渲染线性一致性/turn 边界原子性/三路 DB reload 修复链）；弱网风险点 V1-V4 + 修复优先级
- `docs/agent/web-linearizability.md` — **Web 前端形式化证明（Raft 模型）**: 状态 S=(M,P,L,W)；不变量 I1-I7 + 引理 L1-L7 + 定理 T1-T7（渲染线性一致性/turn 边界无闪烁/顺序正确/reload 一致性/追赶收敛/gap 修复/跨 turn 隔离）；诚实标注 4 个已知前提违反点 V1-V4（修复前最终一致，修复后无条件线性一致）
- `docs/agent/genui-plugin-design.md` — **GenUI (display_html) 插件化设计**: display_html 已从内置工具迁移为独立 Go stdio channel 插件 `plugins/xbot-genui/`（channel_tools 声明 + `channels:["web"]` + `ui` 元数据）。通用性设计 §9（UI 能力由工具元数据声明，不由工具名决定 —— `tools.UIDecl`/`tools.UIDeclProvider` 接口，engine_wire 流式提取 + 前端渲染判定全部读元数据）+ 形式化证明 §10 T1-T9（正确性 + 流畅性）。前端 XBOT_UI 运行时（`web/src/genui/runtime.tsx`：组件库/ECharts/three/motion/主题）。**前端 GenUI 渲染已迁移到 messageRenderer 声明**：`PluginRuntime.renderTool` 调度器（`matchesTool` 匹配 `{tool}`/`{uiMode}`/`{role}`/`{}` + priority 降序 + null fallback）+ 内置 `builtinGenuiRenderer`（matches `{uiMode:'genui'}`）+ `builtinLegacyDisplayHtmlRenderer`（matches `{tool:'display_html'}` 兜底旧历史消息），注册于 `AgentPanel`；`ToolRender`/`AssistantMessage`/`FoldedToolGroup` 改经 renderTool 派发，`genui.ts` 的 `isGenUITool` 删除 `name==='display_html'` fallback（只留 `uiMode` 判据）。`ChannelToolDecl.Channels/UI` 字段 + `ChannelToolBridge` 实现 UIDeclProvider + `execute_tool` result 支持 `ui_code`。**插件 channel 激活需要 `channels.<name>.enabled=true`（config.json）**——`stdioChannelPluginProvider.IsEnabled(nil)` 返回 false，仅装插件不创建 channel 实例，display_html 工具不可见。

## Gotchas — MUST READ Before Any Code Change

### Concurrency
- **Loop breaker（iteration-loop detection）默认关闭（实验开关，2026-08-31 起）——必须在 config.json `agent.experimental.iteration_loop_detection: true` 显式开启。** 接线：`config.ExperimentalConfig.IterationLoopDetection` → `server_core.go` 传 `agent.Config.IterationLoopDetection` → `Agent.iterationLoopDetection` → `buildMainRunConfig`/`buildSubAgentRunConfig` 填 `RunConfig.IterationLoopDetection` → `newRunState` 初始化 `runState.loopDetectionEnabled`。`detectIterationLoop` 开头 gate（`s.loopDetectionEnabled && ...`）：关闭时不置位 `loopDetected`（拦截/loopBreakCount/loopFatal 全链路休眠），但**签名追踪（lastIterSignature）照常更新**（无状态、开销为零，开关切换即时生效）。开启后的行为（下述）不变。**Loop breaker 绝不插入 user message —— 重复调用被替换成挂真实 tool_call_id 的 fake tool result。** `detectIterationLoop` 检测到连续迭代 content+tool_calls 逐字节相同时只置 `runState.loopDetected`；`executeToolCalls` 据此走 `fakeLoopToolResults`：**不执行重复工具**，为每个 tool_call 构造 `Error: ⚠️ LOOP DETECTED` 的 tool result（配对 assistant 的真实 tool_call_id → SanitizeMessages 保留 → LLM 下一迭代看到警告）。旧实现直接 append `Role:"user"` 假消息 → 增量持久化进 DB（role=user, turn_id=当前 turn）→ web 前端按 (turnID, role) 分组渲染时**假 user 行顶替用户真实消息**（用户报告的严重 bug）。fake result 的 `err==nil`（`lastIterHadError` 不置位）→ 模型无视警告继续重复时持续拦截；上一迭代真实工具报错则不拦截（重试合理）。Test: `TestDetectIterationLoop_DisabledByDefault`（默认关闭不拦截）+ `TestDetectIterationLoop_ReplacesDuplicateCallsWithFakeToolResult` 等 4 个开启路径测试（构造 runState 时显式 `loopDetectionEnabled: true` / RunConfig `IterationLoopDetection: true`）。
- **Never `defer` semaphore release inside a loop.** Deadlock when iterations exceed capacity. Release immediately after Generate completes.
- Non-blocking channel sends: always use `select` with `ctx.Done()` to prevent blocking on full channels during shutdown.
- **User-scoped semaphores must not be hardcoded to capacity 1 when one sender can own multiple independent chats/sessions (for example remote CLI windows authenticated as `admin`).** Size them from configured concurrency or key them by session, otherwise different windows will block each other and look like a leaked semaphore.
- **`SetMaxConcurrency` must clear `userSemaphores` cache.** The global semaphore is rebuilt with the new capacity, but `getUserSemaphore` caches per-user channels in a `sync.Map` via `LoadOrStore`. Without `Clear()`, users with custom LLM keep using the cached semaphore with the OLD capacity forever. Symptom: setting max_concurrency to 100 has no visible effect.
- **`cancelChildSessions` must only cancel sessions with matching `parentKey`.** The old code called `cancelCurrent()` on ALL interactive sessions inside the `Range` loop before checking `parentKey`. This killed sibling/peer background agents when any single agent was unloaded or panicked — all N peer agents die simultaneously at whatever time the first one finishes. The `parentKey` check must happen BEFORE `cancelCurrent()`.
- **SubAgents must never outlive their direct creator — ALL completion paths need `cancelChildSessions`.** Foreground SubAgents wrap their own `fgRunCtx` (with `bgSessionCtxKey`+`bgParentKey`) so children derive context from the creator, not a grandparent. Send path `runCtx` also has these markers. Every natural completion path (foreground, bg, send) calls `runCancel()` + `cancelChildSessions(key)`. Without the context wrapper, a foreground B creating bg C would have C's context chain skip B entirely → B finishes, C keeps running.

### Subscription & Settings
- **Multi-user system REMOVED (v63): single operator + admin allowlist.** The canonical user layer (users/user_identities/link_codes tables, IdentityResolver, owner_user_id columns) was deleted. There is exactly ONE operator: `ResolveUserContext` collapses every sender to the fixed operator identity (`cli_user`) — subscriptions, settings, workspace, memory are GLOBAL. Admin rights come from `Agent.isAdminSender(channel, senderID)`: `cli`/`web` channels are always admin (local operator / password login), every other channel (feishu/qq/...) requires the senderID in `config agent.admins`. `RunConfig.OriginUserIsAdmin` is the precomputed decision consumed by the tool layer.
- **Management commands and tool actions are operator-gated.** `protocol.CommandInfo.AdminOnly` + `commandRequiresAdmin` gate both dispatch points (processMessage + chatWorker): `/set-llm /unset-llm /llm /llms /models /set-model /settings /usage /plugin /app` reject non-admin senders. The config tool's `model/subscription/runner/reload_*` actions and ManageTools' `add_mcp/remove_mcp/reload` check `ctx.OriginUserIsAdmin`. Conversational commands (`/new /compress /goal /info /export /bang`) stay open.
- **DB v63 migration** (`migrateV62ToV63`, tests in `storage/sqlite/migration_v63_test.go`): UNIQUE conflict resolution (operator's row wins) → sender collapse to `cli_user` (incl. `user_llm_subscriptions.sender_id` — the `List()` query dimension) → token usage SUM-merged → xbot memories `user_id=1` (column KEPT — memory scopeWhere reads it) → DROP canonical columns (`user_llm_subscriptions/user_settings/user_default_model/user_chats/cron_jobs/event_triggers .user_id`, `tenants/runners .owner_user_id`; `idx_cron_jobs_user` dropped first) → DROP `users/user_identities/link_codes`. Every step guarded by `tableExists` (hand-built migration-test fixtures may lack tables) + `HAVING COUNT(*)>0` on token-usage aggregates (empty set emits a NULL row violating NOT NULL).
- **`Agent.ResolveUserContext` is the SINGLE boundary between the (removed) user system and the agent loop.** It resolves LLM/settings/sandbox/concurrency components once at `processMessage` entry, carried via `context.Context` (`WithUserContext`/`UserContextFromContext`). The admin decision (`isAdminSender`) runs on the ORIGINAL channel+sender BEFORE the operator collapse. **NEVER access `a.llmFactory`/`a.settingsSvc` directly in agent loop code** — read from `UserContext` closures (`uc.SubSvc`, `uc.SettingsSvc`, `uc.ResolveLLM(chatID)`, `uc.SelectModel(chatID, subID, model)`, `uc.RefreshModels()`, `uc.InvalidateLLM()`, `uc.HasCustomLLM()`).
- **`agent/setting_runtime.go` is the SINGLE source of truth for runtime setting handlers.** Both CLI and server use it. Never create a second handler registry — it will silently diverge.
- **`backendSubscriptionManager` is the SINGLE SubscriptionManager implementation.** No more local/remote/config variants. All subscription operations go through Backend interface → Transport (local or remote).
- **Never use `UpdateSubscription` for PerModelConfig changes.** Use `UpdatePerModelConfig(subID, model, pmc)` — it only touches PerModelConfigs, never touches credentials. The old List→modify→Update pattern reads masked keys from the API, then writes them back, destroying real credentials.
- **`serverapp/rpc_table.go:updateSubscription` starts from EXISTING subscription, only overlays non-masked fields.** Client sends masked keys (****) — the handler preserves real credentials from DB.
- **`max_context_tokens` is per-model, NOT per-subscription.** Stored in `PerModelConfigs[model].MaxContext`, NOT in user_settings DB or config.Agent.MaxContextTokens. `/settings` writes only the current session's model via `UpdatePerModelConfig`, preserving existing `MaxOutputTokens`. The old `SetMaxContextTokens` / `set_max_context_tokens` RPC / `perChatMaxCtx` map / `max_context_tokens` setting handler have all been REMOVED — settings writes go directly to DB via `UpdatePerModelConfig`, and `GetEffectiveMaxContext` reads from DB via `resolveSubContextFor`. Must NOT pass `max_context_tokens` to `ApplySettings` — there is no handler for it anymore.
- **`thinking_mode` is a GLOBAL user setting, NOT subscription-scoped ("订阅是订阅，模型是模型").** Stored in `user_settings` under canonical channel `channel.ThinkingModeChannel == "cli"` (constant lives in the `channel` package to avoid the `channel/cli ↔ agent` import cycle). Surfaced via the main chat status-bar indicator (`🧠 auto|on|off [Ctrl+M]`, `renderReadyStatus` + `thinkingZoneXStart/End` click zone) and `Ctrl+M` toggle (`toggleThinkingMode` cycles `""→enabled→disabled→""`). `ResolveLLM` reads it via `f.userThinkingMode(senderID)` — priority per-model override (`pmc.thinkingMode`) → global user setting → `""`. `sub.ThinkingMode` is NO LONGER read (kept only as an internal fallback field). The `thinking_mode` `SettingHandlerRegistry` handler calls `f.InvalidateSender(senderID)` (no-op now — no in-memory cache to clear; DB is read fresh every turn). `cmd/xbot-cli/main.go` `ApplySettings` must NOT treat `thinking_mode` as an llm field (`updateActiveSubscription` no longer writes `sub.ThinkingMode`) — thinking flows only through `ApplyRuntimeSettings` → handler → `InvalidateSender`. Startup seeds the global user setting once (`serverapp`: `cfg.LLM.ThinkingMode` → default-sub `ThinkingMode` → `""`). Subscription add/edit/display panels are credentials-only (name/provider/base_url/api_key); `setLLMCmdForSub` shows only credential params.
- **`refreshRemoteValuesCache` MUST extract `max_context_tokens` from `PerModelConfigs[sub.Model].MaxContext`.** The function populates `valuesCache` which feeds `GetCurrentValues()` → `resolveMaxContext()` → context bar display. Without this extraction, the cache falls through to the `config.DefaultMaxContextTokens` (200000) fallback, causing the context bar to show wrong max context after settings save. The extraction must mirror the settings panel read path (`cli_settings.go:79-93`): PerModelConfigs[model].MaxContext → sub.MaxContext → config fallback. Also must NOT overwrite existing DB values from `GetSettings` (config tool wrote them via `SetSetting`).
- **`SwitchModel` with chatID must NOT call `SetModel` on the subscription.** Per-session model switches (with chatID) only create per-chat cache entries. Calling `SetModel` contaminates all sessions sharing the same subscription. Only user-level switches (no chatID) update the subscription.
- **`PerModelConfigs` is a read-side projection populated by the storage layer (v42).** `subscription_models` is the sole source for per-model config; the `per_model_configs` JSON column was dropped. `LLMSubscriptionService.List`/`Get`/`GetDefault` call `loadPerModelConfigs` (which reads `subscription_models`) AFTER closing the rows cursor — a nested query inside `rows.Next()` deadlocks the single-connection SQLite pool. `Add` upserts the incoming map into the table; `UpdatePerModelConfigs` does delete+reinsert. The old `mergeSubscriptionModels` in rpc_table was deleted (no longer needed). Client-side `ResolveEffectiveMaxContext`/`sub.PerModelConfigs` now see table values without an explicit merge step.
- **`IsDefault`/`Active` are read-side projections derived from `user_default_model` (v43).** The `is_default` column was dropped. `GetDefault` reads `user_default_model` → `Get(subID)` and marks `IsDefault=true`; `List`/`ListAll` mark via `markDefaultsFor`/`markDefaultsAll`. `SetDefault(id)` upserts `user_default_model`. `Add`/`Update` seed/sync `user_default_model` when `IsDefault=true`. `Get` does NOT populate `IsDefault` (stays false) — callers needing default-status use `GetDefault`/`List` or check `user_default_model` directly (e.g. `updateSubscription` RPC re-switches the user-level entry only if `GetUserDefaultModel` matches the edited sub).
- **System subscription REMOVED (v62); defaultLLM is the in-memory cfg.LLM fallback; models never travel alone (model-subscription integration).** The v44 system subscription (`id="system"`, `is_system=1`) was deleted — it was a bug farm (guard-matrix holes, list-order divergence, priority conflicts, `SetSystemLLM` ≡ `SetDefaults`, `defaultLLM` polluted by cli_user's personal subscription in multi-user deployments). Since v62: (1) `defaultLLM` is built ONCE from `cfg.LLM` (config.json + env) at `NewLLMFactory` time and is NEVER re-pointed (`SwitchSubscription` is a no-op hook; `SetSystemLLM`/`SetDefaults`/`SetConfigSubs`/`buildModelSubscriptionMap`/`configSubToLLMSubscription`/`ResolveSubIDForModel` are all deleted); it is a deployment-level fallback — not in DB, not in the picker, not selectable. (2) `GetDefault` returns `(nil, nil)` when the operator has no default — no system fallback. (3) `List`/`ListAll` no longer inject any shared row (the ByUserID variants were removed with the multi-user system in v63); `GetLLM` falls through to `defaultLLM`. (4) **Model-subscription integration invariant**: persisted/protocol-layer model references ALWAYS carry their owning subscription — tier values are strictly `"subID|model"` (`SetUserTierModel` rejects bare names), `get_default_model` RPC returns a `{sub_id, model}` object (`Client.GetDefaultModelPair`), `Agent.SetUserModel` requires subID, `iteration_history` carries `subscription_id` (v62 column; `GetTenantUsageStats` by_model groups by `(subscription_id, model)`), web `createSession` passes `subscription_id` alongside `model`, `GetLLMForModel` returns `(client, subID, model, ...)` as a pair (no `resolveSubIDForModel` second pass). Bare model names are allowed ONLY at human-input boundaries (SubAgent `model` param, tier names) and are resolved exactly once via `ResolveSubscriptionForModel` — the single input resolver. `GetLLMForModel` has NO "any subscription + arbitrary model name" hard-tries: a model without an owning subscription falls back to `defaultLLM` with a warning. The v62 migration deletes the system row + all references (user_default_model/tenants/subscription_models/user_identities), rewrites `system|model` tier values via owner re-resolution (unresolvable → cleared), and DROPs the `is_system` column (DB backed up to `*.pre-v62.bak` first via `VACUUM INTO`).
- **`handleSwitchLLMDoneMsg` must NOT pass `done.maxCtx` as `state.MaxContextTokens`.** Those values come from `resolveSubMaxContext` using the subscription's DEFAULT model. Setting them poisons `ResolveEffectiveMaxContext` priority-1 shortcut, bypassing per-model lookup. Let `applySessionLLMState` resolve dynamically from `PerModelConfigs`.
- **`scheduleSessionLLMRestore` must use `m.cachedModelName` (per-session model from `refreshCachedModelName`), NOT `target.Model` (subscription default).** The old code always used `target.Model`, so per-session model choices (Ctrl+N switch) were lost on restart — the restored LLM used the subscription's default model. `handleSwitchLLMDoneMsg` must also call `SwitchModel(senderID, done.subModel, chatID)` AFTER `SetDefault(subID, chatID)`, because `SetDefault`→`SetSessionLLM` creates the per-chat entry with `sub.Model` (subscription default), overriding the per-session model. The `SwitchModel` call corrects the model field and re-persists to the tenants table.
- **`OnModelsLoaded` callback runs in `NewOpenAILLM`'s async goroutine** — must be concurrency-safe.
- **Tier fallback**: unconfigured tier → vanguard→balance→swift chain. Empty tier must NOT return default client with wrong model.
- **`createClientFromSub` uses sub's credentials with a *different* model** — verify target model is served by that endpoint.
- **Model-first redesign (v39): `ResolveLLM` is the authoritative agent-loop entry.** It reads **DB directly** every call (no in-memory cache): ProxyLLM → tenants table → `user_default_model` → legacy `GetLLM` fallback. The old `sessionMemo`/`entries`/`hasCustomLLMCache`/`perChatMaxCtx` in-memory caches have been REMOVED — `Invalidate`/`InvalidateSender`/`InvalidateSession` are now no-ops (DB is authoritative), `InvalidateAll` only clears `clientCache`. `GetLLMForChat` reads per-session (subID, model) from tenants table, falls back to `GetLLM` (global default). `SetSessionLLM` writes to tenants table via `tenantSvc.SetTenantSubscription`. New LLM features must build on `ResolveLLM`/`SelectModel`, not the removed caches. See `docs/agent/subscription.md` "Model-First Redesign".
- **Cross-subscription model switch 404: `switch_model` per-session must resolve the owner subscription, not use `GetDefault()`.** The old code paired `(defaultSub.ID, anyModel)` into tenants — switching to a model from another subscription built a client from the default sub's baseURL/apiKey but requested the other model name → 404 "model not supported by any configured account". Fix: `ResolveSubscriptionForModel(senderID, model)` finds the enabled subscription that provides the model, then `SelectModel(owner.ID, model, chatID)`. Fallback to default-sub only when no owner resolves.
- **`clientCache` (keyed by `(subID, apiType)`) must be invalidated on every subscription CRUD.** `add/remove/update/set_default/set_subscription_model/update_per_model_config` RPCs and `LLMAdd/Remove/SetDefault/Update` callbacks must call `LLMFactory().InvalidateSubscription(subID)`, or `ResolveLLM` reuses a client built from stale credentials.
- **Disabled models (`subscription_models.enabled=0`) are excluded from `ListAllModelsForUser` and rejected by `SelectModel`/`ResolveSubscriptionForModel`.** `protocol.PerModelConfig.Enabled` is a read-side projection (populated by `mergeSubscriptionModels`); writes go through the `set_model_enabled` RPC, never through `UpdatePerModelConfig`.
- **v39/v40 migration notes.** `schemaVersion=40` (v40 added `user_llm_subscriptions.enabled`). A live DB that reached v38 via the pre-rebase model-pool binary lacks `runner_id` and needs a manual `ALTER TABLE tenants ADD COLUMN runner_id TEXT DEFAULT ''` before running the new binary (dev-machine-only; not in migration code).

### Append-Only History & Rewind (v52+)
- **`session_messages` is an append-only history log.** All content mutations (compress, context_edit, mask, AskUser Q&A) are appended as control records (`record_type` != `message`); current state is reconstructed by `SessionService.Replay()`. Physical DELETE only on rewind (`RewindToHistoryID`) and clear (`Clear`). `PurgeOldMessages` 已彻底删除（原 no-op 死链：agent/config/storage/session/serverapp 全链路移除）。
- **`ChatMessage.ID` (int64, `json:"-"`) is the stable DB auto-increment id** — used for rewind, trim, dedup, and append-only history node tracking. Never sent to the LLM. PR #256 originally named this `HistoryID`; it was unified to master's `ID` during the rebase. `HistoryMessage` (protocol) carries both `ID` (`json:"id"`) and `HistoryID` (`json:"history_id"`) — they are the same value, kept for client compatibility.
- **`appendMessageWith` MUST include `turn_id` in the INSERT.** The v50 migration added `turn_id` for turn-scoped dedup. `AddMessageWithID` delegates to `AppendMessage` (which calls `appendMessageWith`) — both acquire the striped `historyLock`. Never write to `session_messages` without holding `historyLock(tenantID)`.
- **Compound history writes use `withImmediateHistoryWrite` (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE`).** `AppendMessages`, `AppendMessagesAndAskQuestion`, `AppendControl`, `AppendContextSnapshot`, `RewindToHistoryID`, `ReplaceToolMessage` all use it. Do NOT use `conn.Begin()` (deferred tx) for history writes — it risks `SQLITE_BUSY` under the 4-connection pool.
- **`rewind` 必须按 `turn_id` 删除 `iteration_history`，不能只按 `message_id`。** `writeIterationHistory`（engine_run_tools.go）写入 iteration_history 时 `message_id=0`（读侧按 `turn_id` 查询，不关联具体消息）。旧版 `RewindToHistoryID` 只 `DELETE ... WHERE message_id IN (SELECT id FROM session_messages WHERE id >= ?)` —— `message_id=0` 永远匹配不到（session_messages.id 从 1 起），残留。server 重启后 `restoreTurnIDSeq` 从 DB 恢复 turn_id（复用被删的 turn_id）→ `GetIterationHistoryByTurn` 查到残留旧 iterations → 两个 turn 的 iterations 混在一起（用户报告："两个 agent turn 混一起，user1 消失"）。修复：删除条件改为 `turn_id IN (SELECT turn_id FROM session_messages WHERE id >= ? AND turn_id > 0) OR message_id IN (...)`。Test: `TestRewindDeletesIterationHistoryByTurnID`。
- **Session operation gate serializes Run + Rewind.** `chatProcessLoop` uses blocking `lock(ctx)`; `RewindHistory` uses non-blocking `tryLock()` (fails immediately if a Run is active). After DB truncation, in-memory caches (`lastProgressSnapshot`, `iterationHistories`, `streamState`, `PendingAskUser`) are cleared BEFORE the gate releases. The `history_rewound` session event is emitted while the gate is still held — it's a route-scoped transport barrier.
- **`broadcastHistoryResetLocked` unlocks `seqMu` internally** (mid-function, after `sequenceEventLocked`). The caller `broadcastSessionState` must NOT `defer seqMu.Unlock()` — it would double-unlock. The function acquires `seqMu.Lock()`, calls `broadcastHistoryResetLocked` (which unlocks), then returns.
- **Frontend `progressStore.lastIter` starts at `-1`** (not `0`). This ensures the first structured event (`iteration: 1`) does NOT clear stream-only fields (`streamContent`, `reasoningStreamContent`) — `hadPreviousIteration = lastIter >= 0` is false for the first iteration. Master used `0`; PR #256's `progressStore` requires `-1`.
- **Subscription-level enabled (v40) must be skipped in three places.** `user_llm_subscriptions.enabled=0` must be skipped by `ListAllModelsForUser` (both loops), `ResolveSubscriptionForModel` (`find` closure), and rejected by `SelectModel` — or disabling a subscription is cosmetic. `SetSubscriptionEnabled` must call `InvalidateSubscription(subID)`. `protocol.Subscription.Enabled` is a read-side projection from `user_llm_subscriptions.enabled` (via `subToChannel`/`LLMGetSubscription`); writes go through the `set_subscription_enabled` RPC.
- **No subscription switching in the UI; one unified LLM panel.** `cli_panel_quickswitch.go` is a SINGLE panel (opened by `Ctrl+N` / click status-bar model name / palette "Models & Subscriptions") that combines subscriptions + models + add actions in one flat `[]qsRow` list (`qsSection`/`qsSub`/`qsModel`/`qsAddSub`/`qsAddModel`). There is NO separate "subscription" panel and NO `Ctrl+P`/`Ctrl+N` anymore. Subscription rows are management-only (add/disable/delete); model rows switch cross-subscription. The old `SwitchLLM` async + `cliSwitchLLMDoneMsg` path is retained only for startup restore (`scheduleSessionLLMRestore`). Key scheme (command mode, no filter active): `↑↓` navigate (skips section rows), `Enter` = row action (sub→toggle enabled, model→switch, add row→open add panel), `E` edit current row (sub creds / model params), `D` disable-or-delete (model→toggle enabled, sub→delete), `N` add model (prefills owner from cursor row), `S` toggle `quickSwitchShowAll`, `/` enter filter mode, `Esc` close. **`/` toggles filter mode** so command letters (`e`/`d`/`n`/`s`) never collide with typing — this replaced the old Ctrl-combo commands (`Ctrl+E`/`Ctrl+A`/`Ctrl+T`) which conflicted with global `Ctrl+E` (fold) and `Ctrl+T` (sessions). `applyQuickSwitch` reads `quickSwitchRows[cursor]` and **rejects `Status=="disabled"`** models (keeps panel open, hints press `E` to enable); `normal` AND `offline` are both selectable. Both switch paths use `applyModelSwitch`, which after `SwitchModel` re-reads the owner subscription via `GetSessionSubscription` to fix `activeSubID`/context limits (tenants table is source of truth in both local and remote modes). Status bar shows `模型名(订阅名)` from `cachedSubName` (refreshed by `refreshCachedSubName` on `activeSubID` changes — NOT per-frame). `handleQuickSwitchKey` intercepts Esc/Up/Down/Enter over `quickSwitchRows` BEFORE other handling; in filter mode letters feed `quickSwitchFilterInput` and `rebuildLLMRows` re-filters. **Panel scrolling**: `quickSwitchScrollY` tracks vertical scroll offset; `ensureQuickSwitchCursorVisible` keeps cursor in view; `viewQuickSwitch` slices rows to `height - overhead` visible lines with a `── N-M / Total ──` scroll indicator. **Contextual hints**: `buildQuickSwitchHint()` renders row-type-specific operation hints (sub/model/add/section) in `PanelHint` style + common keys in `TextMutedSt`. **Model selection**: `✓` mark and `cursorToActiveLLMRow` match both `Model` AND `SubID == activeSubID` — same model name from different subscriptions is NOT a match. **Panel colors**: non-cursor rows use `TextSecondarySt` (not `TextMutedSt`); section headers use `PanelDivider`; disabled/offline models use `TextMutedSt`; cursor row uses `Accent`. **Thinking indicator**: `thinkingModeLabel()` returns ASCII `think`/`think+`/`think-` (no emoji, no `[Ctrl+M]` suffix); `think+` uses `Accent` style, `think-` uses `TextMutedSt`.
- **Model list is DB-driven; each entry has a Status (normal/offline/disabled).** `listModelEntriesCore` lists the **union** of `sub.CachedModels` + `sub.Model` + `subscription_models` rows — i.e. everything recorded in the DB, not just fetched. `Status` per (SubID,Model): `disabled` if `subscription_models.enabled=0`; else `normal` if in `CachedModels` or is `sub.Model`; else `offline` (has a record but not fetched — manually added, still selectable). `ListAllModelEntriesForUser` (includeDisabled=true, picker) emits all three; `ListAllModelsForUser` (includeDisabled=false, tier selectors) emits `normal`+`offline` only. Owner attribution picks the best sub by status rank (normal>offline>disabled). The `CachedModels` snapshot is populated by the `OnModelsLoaded` callback — which the model-first refactor had **dropped** (must be re-wired via `makeOnModelsLoaded(subID)` in `createClientFromSub` + both entry-construction paths; the callback nil-checks `Get(subID)` before `UpdateCachedModels` since the latter nil-derefs on missing sub). Opening the panel (`openLLMPanel`) kicks off `RefreshModelEntries()` → RPC `refresh_model_entries` → `LLMFactory.RefreshModelEntriesForUser`: parallel (cap 8, 8s/sub, soft-fail) `/models` fetch per enabled subscription, persist via `OnModelsLoaded`, return fresh entries (a fetched model flips `offline`→`normal`). CLI renders the DB snapshot immediately, shows `↻ 刷新模型列表…`, and rebuilds rows on `cliModelEntriesRefreshedMsg` (handled at top of `Update`). `rebuildLLMRows` clamps the cursor (never resets) so a background refresh doesn't yank the user's cursor; callers set it explicitly (`cursorToActiveLLMRow` on open, `0` while typing). `backendModelLister.EnsureModelsLoaded` is a no-op — refresh is driven by `RefreshModelEntries`, don't rely on `EnsureModelsLoaded`. The three entry points (Ctrl+N / click model name / palette Enter) must drain `m.pendingCmds` so the refresh cmd actually runs. **`/models` per-provider differences are expected** (different providers expose different model lists); the panel shows all DB records regardless, `N` lets users register models the provider didn't list (appears as `offline`), and `isNoiseModel` hides chat-unusable noise (image/realtime/whisper/tts/audio/embed/moderation/dated-snapshots) unless `S` shows all. **Per-model params are edited ONLY on the model row via `E`** (`openEditModelPanel` → `UpdatePerModelConfig`/`SetModelEnabled`); the subscription edit panel (`openEditSubscriptionPanel`) edits credentials/defaults only and NO longer carries per-model toggles — that was redundant with the model row editor.

### Context Management & Token Estimation
- **`maybeCompress` uses ONLY API-returned `prompt_tokens` via `TokenTracker.GetPromptTokens()`.** No local estimation (tiktoken/CountMessagesTokens) in the compression decision path. The `no_data` source (no API data yet) skips all compress/masking checks. Tests must set `cfg.LastPromptTokens` to simulate a previous Run. **`maybeCompress` does NOT add `completionTokens` to its check — `shouldCompact(totalTokens, promptBudget, threshold)` compares `prompt_tokens` against `(maxContextTokens - maxOutputTokens) * threshold`, pure prompt tokens only.** Both the context bar and maybeCompress use the same `tokenTracker.GetPromptTokens()` source — they never diverge.
- **⚠️ 压缩三把尺子 + 无限循环三重防线（2026-08-30 "200k 上下文无限循环压缩"修复）。** 触发用【真实 API prompt_tokens】vs `0.9×(maxContext−maxOutput)`；压缩产出（compactMessages）按 maxContext 全量预算；**post-compress 达标检查必须用 `estimateMessagesTokens`（全量估算：system+summary+tail+tool args，chars×2/3，trigger.go）**——曾经用 `CompressedTokens`（仅摘要估算，不含 system/tail）→ 假达标 → aggressiveTruncate 安全网永不触发 → 每 5 迭代白烧一次全上下文压缩 LLM 调用（200k + 98k maxOutput 触发线仅 91.8k，不可压缩基座轻松超线）。三重防线：① 达标检查（无 `len>10` guard——消息少但单条巨大也要处理）→ 截断 → 截断后复估仍超限或无可截断 → `compressAbandoned=true`（本轮 Run 熔断，Error log + compressWarning 指导调 max_context/max_output）；② `maybeCompress` 无收益熔断：连续 2 次触发时真实值降幅 <5%（`lastCompressTriggerTokens` 同口径对比，防 chars×2/3 对中文低估的假阴性）→ 熔断；③ 错误驱动路径（`handleInputTooLong`/`context_window_exceeded` 的 `maxCompressRetries=3`）**不受** `compressAbandoned` 影响。`setTokenUsageAfterCompress`/`SaveContextTokens`/`SaveTokenState`/handleInputTooLong 全部用全量估算（DB 恢复值语义 = 压缩后上下文规模）。有效压缩（降幅 ≥5%）永不熔断（计数 reset）。Tests: `agent/compress_infinite_loop_test.go` 4 例（假达标截断/不可收缩熔断/连续无效熔断/有效压缩对照）。
- **⚠️ xbot-memory 的 LLM 调用必须流式（`m.generateLLM`，2026-08-30 "PostCompress 卡 10 分钟"修复）——三个调用点（updateCoreSummary/generateSessionSummary/extractAtomicMemories）曾直调 `llmClient.Generate`（非流式）。** 非流式走 `perAttemptCtx` 的 120s 硬 deadline：压缩成功后 PostCompress 的 `updateCoreSummary`（core summary 更新，输入=当前摘要+高重要性记忆）在慢集群上 120s 内回不来 → 5 次重试全烧（turn 367 现场：16:14:37 压缩 39s 成功 → 16:16:37/16:18:38 attempt 1/2 超时……用户看到"压缩又卡了"——卡的是 PostCompress 不是压缩本体）；PreCompress 的记忆提取同理（15:03-15:58 的 8 次 deadline 重试风暴）。修复：`generateLLM` 辅助（xbot.go，SetLLM 旁）——RetryLLM→`GenerateStreamAndCollect`（整周期重试）/ StreamingLLM→`GenerateStream`+`CollectStream`（**idle timeout 每 chunk 重置，无总 deadline**）/ 非流式 client 兜底 `Generate`（测试 mock 兼容）；三个调用点全部替换。与 compactMessages `Stream:true`（同日修复，同根因：非流式 120s 硬 deadline）构成完整链路：**PreCompress → 压缩本体 → PostCompress 全流式**。Tests: `memory/xbot/xbot_stream_test.go`（红→绿：调用点走流式 + streamed tool_calls 收集 + 非流式 fallback）。
- **`ResetAfterCompress()` takes no arguments — it zeros ALL tracker fields** (promptTokens, completionTokens, hadLLMCall). Any non-zero value causes `maybeCompress` to re-trigger immediately, creating infinite compression loops. The tracker returns "no_data" until the next real LLM API call.
- **`RecordLLMCall(prompt, completion)` takes 2 args only.** No msgCount — the old delta estimation via `CountMessagesTokens` has been removed. Offload handles large tool results; a single iteration cannot add enough tokens to justify local estimation.
- **`RecordLLMCall` must NOT be called when `response.Usage.PromptTokens == 0`.** When a stream is cancelled mid-flight (Ctrl+C), `CollectStreamWithCallback` returns a non-nil response with zero Usage (usage events arrive at stream end). Calling `RecordLLMCall(0, 0)` overwrites the tracker's correct value from a previous iteration. This causes `SaveState` to skip (`promptTokens <= 0`), but `SaveTokenState(0, 0)` and `SaveContextTokens(0)` in `callLLM` still execute unconditionally, overwriting DB with 0. Next turn restores 0 → context bar empty → first callLLM corrects to real value → immediate compression. Fix: guard all three save paths (`RecordLLMCall`, `SaveContextTokens`, `SaveTokenState`) with `response.Usage.PromptTokens > 0`.
- **`buildToolContextExtras` uses `TenantSession.MemoryService()` for `MemorySvc`/`TenantID`, NOT `LettaMemory` type assertion.** These are tenant-level fields that work for all memory providers. Only LettaMemory-specific fields (CoreMemory, ArchivalMemory, ToolIndexer) stay inside the type assertion.
- **`ObservationMaskStore` and `OffloadStore` both persist to disk.** Mask uses `~/.xbot/mask/{tenantID}/{id}.json`, Offload uses `~/.xbot/offload_store/{session}/{id}.json`. `Recall` falls back to disk on memory miss. Both cleaned on compress and `/clear`. `calculateKeepGroups` 档位：100/80/50/30（按 token ratio ≤0.70/0.80/0.90/else），放宽自旧值 12/8/5/3——正常会话几乎不触发 masking。Test: `TestIntegration_Masking_TriggeredAtThreshold`（120 groups + maxTokens=9000 → keepGroups=100, mask 20）。
- **`PersistenceBridge` manages the persistence watermark (`lastPersistedCount`), not inline fields.** All compression paths use `ApplyCompress` pipeline which calls `PersistenceBridge.RewriteAfterCompress()` to atomically clear+rewrite+update watermark. The invariant `LastPersistedCount <= len(messages)` is verified by `ValidateInvariants()` at debug level after every LLM call, compression, and persistence operation.
- **`handleRunOutput`/`handleCancelledRun` must use `out.IterationHistory` directly, NOT `mergeIterationHistory`.** `mergeIterationHistory` was designed to fill pre-restart iterations from `a.iterationHistories` (in-memory). But `a.iterationHistories` is NOT restored from DB on restart — it's always empty after restart — so the merge was always a no-op in the restart case. The ONLY case where it triggered was the AskUser continuation: `a.iterationHistories` persists across Runs in the same turn (WaitingUser does NOT delete it), so `len(global) > len(local)` → the merge returns ALL iterations (first Run + second Run). But the first Run's iterations are ALREADY persisted to a histMsg at WaitingUser time (`buildWaitingUserOutbound`). Including them again in the final reply's Detail → `ConvertMessagesToHistory` renders them twice (once in histMsg, once in final reply). Fix: use `out.IterationHistory` (current Run's snapshots only) directly in both `handleRunOutput` and `handleCancelledRun`. The restart case is handled by the histMsg (persisted to DB, restored via `ConvertMessagesToHistory`'s Detail parsing).
- **`reconcileHistoryWithLiveRows` must dedup by `turnID:role`, not `eventSeq`/`content`.** After cancel, `appendAssistant` creates a live message (`seq-N`, `turnID=3`, `persisted=false`, `content="streaming text"`). `markDestructiveMutation` → next reload uses `reconcileHistoryWithLiveRows`. DB returns `[interrupted]` (`hist-N`, `turnID=3`, `content=""`). The old code checked `historyHasSeq(cancel_seq)` (false — DB seq differs) and `historyHasContent('assistant', "streaming text")` (false — DB content is `""` for `[interrupted]`). Both checks failed → the live row survived → **duplicate rendering**. Fix: build a `Set<turnID:role>` from history. If the live message's `turnID:role` is already in the set, drop it. Keep `content:role` fallback for `turnID=0` messages (user_echo). Test: `cancel: appendAssistant with turnID does NOT duplicate after reload`. **Reload 主路径 ALWAYS reconcile（不是 mutated 才 reconcile）**：`markDestructiveMutation`（cancel）在 reload **前**递增 gen，reload 捕获的 mutationGen 已含标记 → `mutated=false` → 直接 `parsed` 替换会把 persisted user_echo 行丢光（DB 快照竞态缺它时 user 消息消失，刷新才回来）。**persisted USER 行保护规则**：user_echo 行 eventSeq=undefined（web_inbound.go echo 无 seq），所以 (a) 带 eventSeq 的 echo（SSE replay）走 watermark 判断（`eventSeq < historyWatermark` → 丢弃，否则保留）；(b) 无 eventSeq 的 echo 仅当 `turnID > newestUserTurn`（快照最新 user turn）时保留——竞态 reload 期间当前 turn 的消息保留，同/更早 turn 的旧行被 DB 覆盖（同 session 后台 reload 替换旧内容）。persisted assistant 行永远由 DB 版本权威（丢弃）。
- **Iteration numbers are 1-based across the entire stack.** The engine's Run loop uses `for i := 1; i <= s.maxIter; i++` (not `0..maxIter-1`). `beginIteration(1)` sets `structuredProgress.Iteration = 1`. `snapshotCompletedIteration(1)` writes `Iteration: 1` to Detail JSON. `ConvertMessagesToHistory`'s `curIterIdx` starts at 0, `++` gives 1 (aligned). `reconstructIterationsFromMessages` uses `iterIdx++` starting from 0 (gives 1). `attachIterationDelta`'s guard is `prev.Iteration >= 1` (0 = uninitialized). Frontend `progressStore.lastIter` starts at 0 (uninitialized), `>= 1` triggers the "had previous iteration" clear. DB migration v51 rewrites old Detail JSON from 0-based to 1-based. **0 is always "uninitialized/untracked", never a valid iteration number.**
- **Turn ID is restored from DB on server restart.** `chatProcessLoop` calls `tenantSession.GetMaxTurnID()` at startup and stores it into `ss.turnIDSeq` via `ss.turnIDSeq.Store(maxTurnID)`. This ensures the per-session turn counter stays globally monotonic across restarts — without it, the counter resets to 0 and the next turn gets `turn_id=1`, colliding with a pre-restart turn. `SessionService.GetMaxTurnID(tenantID)` runs `SELECT MAX(turn_id) FROM session_messages WHERE tenant_id = ?`.
- **`ConvertMessagesToHistoryWithIterations` MUST flush pending iterations at the turn boundary.** A restart-interrupted turn has NO final assistant message — its intermediate (tool_calls) messages sit back-to-back with the recovery turn's intermediate messages (restart auto-recovery continues with a NEW turn_id, no user separator). The old code accumulated ALL intermediate messages into ONE `pendingIters` buffer with `pendingTurnID` = last turn; `flushPending` then replaced the WHOLE buffer with the LAST turn's `turnIterMap` records — silently dropping every iteration of the earlier turn from `get_history` (the data was in `iteration_history` the whole time; symptom: pre-restart iterations "vanished" after restart, e.g. fancy memory tenant 134262 turn 219: 13 ih rows existed but only turn 220's 2 rendered). Fix: in the `len(m.ToolCalls) > 0` branch, `if pendingTurnID > 0 && m.TurnID != pendingTurnID { flushPending() }` BEFORE accumulating. `flushPending` also resets `curIterIdx = 0` so fallback iteration numbers restart at 1 per turn. Regression test: `TestConvert_WithIterations_RestartTurnBoundary` (`channel/iteration_history_test.go`).
- **⚠️ 渲染丢迭代第二形态（2026-08-23 15:02 事故，tenant 150660 turn 67→68）：恢复 turn 只有一条空壳 assistant（v55+ 占位行）时，`!isIntermediate` 结构化分支的 `pendingIters = nil` 蒸发上一 turn 的全部迭代。** 现场：重启打断 turn 67（12 对中间消息，无最终 assistant）→ 恢复 turn 68 **没有中间 tool 迭代**（模型直接输出总结）→ 持久化只有一条 `content=""` 的空壳 assistant（v55+ 设计：回复文本存 iteration_history，session_messages 是占位）→ 空壳无 tool_calls **不经过 tool_calls 分支的 turn-boundary flush** → 直接进 `!isIntermediate && m.TurnID > 0` 分支 → `pendingIters = nil` 把 turn 67 的 12 个迭代抹掉，且本分支只渲染 `turnIterMap[68]` → 前端只见 1 个 iter（"重启前的 iter 全部消失"）。**触发条件 = 重启落在 turn 执行中 AND 恢复 turn 无中间迭代**——概率性复现的来源。修复（subscription.go）：该分支入口加 `if pendingTurnID > 0 && m.TurnID != pendingTurnID && len(pendingIters) > 0 { flushPending() }`（跨 turn 先 flush 上一 turn，本 turn 走 turnIterMap 权威渲染）。Test: `TestConvert_WithIterations_ResumeTurnEmptyShellAssistant`（红灯复现 `got 2: [user:67(0iters) assistant:68(1iters)]` → 绿灯）。**与"prompt_tokens 恒定"类事故的区别**：这是渲染层（get_history）丢迭代，DB 的 iteration_history 完好——与 LLM 请求上下文无关。
- **⚠️ 重启 resume 必须复用被中断 turn 的 turn_id + 续接迭代号（resolveResumeTurnID + IterationStart，2026-08-29 "两个大 dom" 修复）——否则同一逻辑 turn 被拆成 user(N)/resume(N+1)/resume(N+2) 三个 turn，前端渲染成多个 assistant 块，与不重启的最终渲染不一致。** 根因：`InjectInboundResume`（`resumePendingTurns` 优雅停机恢复 / `/continue`）的空消息经 `admitToMsgCh` 走通用路径 `nextTurnID()` 分配**新** turn_id——每重启一次就多拆一个 turn（tenant 157491 现场：user=275 + resume1=276 + resume2=277，`turn-276-c` + `turn-277-live` 两个大 DOM）。修复三层：① `admitToMsgCh` 对 `resume_turn` 跳过预分配（与 ask_user_answered 同路径）；② `chatProcessLoop` 出队时 `resolveResumeTurnID`（`GetLastUserTurnID` = 最后一条非 display-only user 消息的 turn，解析失败退回 nextTurnID）复用被中断 turn；TURN_ID 单调性检查与 `lastTurnID` 基线对 resume 豁免（复用 id 来自 DB 非计数器，可能小于基线）；③ 恢复 Run 的迭代号**必须续接**——`RunConfig.IterationStart`（`buildMainRunConfig` 对 resume 查 `GetMaxIterationForTurn`，engine Run 循环 `i := n + s.iterStart`）：迭代号是 turn 域唯一（iteration_history 按 (turn_id, iteration) 关联；前端 committed turn 的遮蔽解除要求 `ev.iter > maxIter`，从 1 重开会被判重放丢弃）。前端零改动（reduce.ts 的 committed 遮蔽解除 + union 合并现成）。Tests: `TestResolveResumeTurnID` / `TestAdmitToMsgCh_ResumeSkipsTurnAllocation` / `TestRun_IterationStart` / `TestGetLastUserTurnID` / `TestGetMaxIterationForTurn` / `TestConvert_WithIterations_ResumedTurnReusesTurnID` / reduce.test.ts「重启 resume 复用同 turn」。
- **Shell binary output silently bypasses offload: `summarizeShell` splits by `\n` and keeps last 5 lines.** Binary data (e.g. `cat libmujoco.so`) has very few newlines, so "last 5 lines" ≈ entire content. The offload summary then contains nearly all original binary data, causing context explosion (39k→2.2M tokens observed). `summarizeShell` now applies `maxLineRunes=500` truncation (same as `summarizeRead`) on output lines. But any new summary generator must handle the few-lines-but-megabytes-per-line case.
- **`SanitizeMessages()` is the final validation gate before sending to LLM.** Five passes: (1) strip assistant messages with empty content AND no tool_calls — rejected by OpenAI/Anthropic; (2) strip tool_calls with invalid/malformed JSON arguments — caused by cancelled stream with partially-generated tool arguments, DeepSeek rejects with 400; (3) strip trailing unpaired tool_calls from cancelled runs; (4) ensure every tool_call_id has a matching tool response message — strip orphaned tool_calls, re-run Pass 1 to clean up emptied assistant messages; (5) strip orphaned tool messages whose tool_call_id doesn't appear in any assistant's tool_calls — Pass 2 and Pass 4 can strip tool_calls leaving orphaned tool messages, DeepSeek rejects with "Messages with role 'tool' must be a response to a preceding message with 'tool_calls'". Called in `buildPrompt` and `engine.go`.
- **`ConvertMessagesToHistory` infers tool status from tool messages when Detail is absent.** When the server crashes mid-turn, persisted assistant messages have ToolCalls but NO Detail (Detail is only set at end-of-turn in `agent_process.go`). The old code hard-coded `Status: "done"` for all tools in this fallback path — failed tools rendered as successful after TUI restart. Fix: pre-scan tool messages to build a `toolCallID → content` map; if content starts with `"Error:"` (the prefix `engine_run_tools.go:updateToolResultLine` adds for `IsError` results), status becomes `"error"`, otherwise `"done"`. `cursorToActiveLLMRow` and the `✓` mark in `viewQuickSwitch` match both `Model` AND `SubID == activeSubID` — same model name from different subscriptions is NOT a match.
- **`CollectStreamWithCallback` must return partial content on `ctx.Done()`, not nil.** When user cancels streaming mid-reasoning, returning nil loses accumulated `reasoning_content` and partial `tool_calls`. The partial tool_calls may have broken JSON arguments, which then pass through `SanitizeMessages` Pass 2 for cleanup. Returning nil also prevents the engine from properly recording the cancellation state.
- **`GenerateStreamAndCollect` must NOT use `perAttemptCtx` for streaming.** A per-attempt deadline binds to the underlying HTTP connection via ctx. The deadline kills active streams mid-generation even when chunks are still flowing (e.g., DeepSeek long reasoning > 120s). Stream timeout is managed by `CollectStreamWithCallback`'s idle timeout (120s without any chunk) — timer resets on every received chunk, so actively-streaming responses never time out.
- **⚠️ Tool-call arguments JSON 损坏双层检测（2026-08-30 "parse args: unexpected end of JSON input" 修复）——网关中段丢 chunk/尾部断流时两层静默放行，agent 收晦涩 JSON 语法错误盲目重试烧 token。** 根因（DB 铁证 `session_messages.tool_arguments` 列）：MoL/sglang 网关 SSE 转发**丢/重 chunk**，两种损坏形态——中段丢失（`{"id": 2", "status"` 单字符错位 → `invalid character '"' after object key:value pair`，**finish_reason 正常到达**，多数派：08-29 全天 14 次）+ 尾部截断（FileCreate 15890 字符戛然而止 → `unexpected end of JSON input`，无 finish_reason，走 1306 静默推断）。诊断方法：`SELECT tool_arguments FROM session_messages WHERE role='tool' AND content LIKE '%parse args%'`（tool_arguments 列存收到的原始损坏 JSON）+ 日志 `Tool failed: parse args:`。修复两层：① `openai.go processStream` 1306 分支（断流+hasToolCalls 曾静默推断 FinishReasonToolCalls）→ `allToolCallArgsValid(toolArgsByIdx)` json.Valid 校验，非法 → EventError "tool call arguments truncated" → RetryLLM 重试；② `stream.go CollectStreamWithCallbackFrom` 完成点（`!ok` 正常关闭分支）`firstInvalidToolCallArgs(resp.ToolCalls)` 校验——**中段损坏 + finish_reason 正常到达的场景只有这层能拦**（openai_responses.go 738 行同款推断分支同修；anthropic.go 天然安全——message_stop 显式结束信号，EOF+hasContent → EventError）。`retry.go isRetryableError` 加关键词 `"tool call arguments corrupted"`（重试可再生完整流，chunk 丢失是瞬态）。**空 arguments（无参工具）合法**；json.Valid 是唯一判据。Tests: `llm/stream_truncation_test.go`（断流 3 例）+ `llm/stream_completion_gate_test.go`（中段损坏+finish_reason 正常 2 例）。**上游真凶在网关侧（pd_router SSE 转发丢 chunk）**——xbot 侧只做检测+重试自愈；网关不修则每次损坏都白烧一轮 LLM 调用后重试成功。
- **MCP stdio `exec.Command` uses process PATH, not `cmd.Env`.** Go's `exec.Command("npx", ...)` resolves the executable using the *process* PATH (`os.Getenv("PATH")`), ignoring `cmd.Env`. When xbot runs as a service with minimal PATH, tools like npx/nvm are invisible. `ConnectStdioServer` now calls `resolveCommand()` to pre-resolve the absolute path using the login shell PATH from `getLoginShellEnv()`. Also, `getLoginShellEnv` uses `bash -i -l` (interactive login shell) because `bash -l` alone skips `.bashrc` where nvm initializes.

- **MCP connections are POOLED, not per-session (tools/mcp_pool.go).** Every session used to own a full `SessionMCPManager` connection set — N sessions × M stdio servers = N×M child processes for identical config (config scope is global + per-sender, NEVER per-session). Now a process-level `MCPConnectionPool` keys entries by the scope 4-tuple (globalConfigPath, userConfigPath, workspaceRoot, userID); `SessionMCPManager` is a thin VIEW (public API unchanged). Three historical bugs fixed: (1) the 30-min inactivity timeout was a DEAD parameter (unload only ran for sessions evicted from the 24h session cache) — the pool reaper now reclaims entries with refCount==0 && idle > 30min every 30s, independent of session caches; (2) unloaded servers never reconnected (`initOnce` stayed 2) — a reaped entry is deleted; the next `Acquire` reconnects; (3) `UpdateScope` disconnected everything on a scope change — it now detaches from the old entry (kept alive for other sharers) and attaches to the new one. `InvalidateAllSessionMCP` wiring calls `GlobalMCPPool().InvalidateAll` directly (one call covers every scope). Tests: `tools/mcp_pool_test.go` (sharing/reaper/refcount/invalidate).

- **`SessionService.Replay` 必须用 `messageIndex` O(1) 查找，禁止对 `result.Messages` 线性扫描。** `GetHistoryBefore`/`GetHistory`（fetchHistory 切换会话时每次调用）全量 Replay。Replay 处理 mask/context_edit 记录时若用 `activeMessageIndexOccurrence`（O(N) 扫描）构成 **O(N²)**：tenant 119384 的 27442 条消息 + 1169 条 mask = 3200 万次扫描 → 秒级延迟 → 前端切换会话时历史过几秒才渲染（只剩 live iter）。修复：Replay 内维护 `messageIndex`（`map[int64][]int` HistoryID → 位置列表），消息追加 `msgIdx.add`、压缩重建 `newMessageIndex(result.Messages)`、查找 `msgIdx.occurrence(id, occ)`/`msgIdx.last(id)`。**禁止在 replayWith 里引入任何对 `result.Messages` 的全量扫描**（性能测试 `TestReplayMaskPerformance`：6000+6000 条 Replay 须 < 2s，实际 38ms）。
- **SQLite `datetime()` comparison with RFC3339 strings is a silent trap.** `created_at` stores `2026-05-24T14:00:00+08:00` (RFC3339 with timezone). `datetime(?, '-2 seconds')` returns `2026-05-24 05:59:58` (UTC, no timezone). Raw string comparison: `'T'` (0x54) > `' '` (0x20) at position 10, so `created_at > datetime(?)` is **always TRUE**, breaking any time-window dedup/filter. Always wrap both sides in `datetime()`: `datetime(created_at) > datetime(?, '-2 seconds')`.

### Cron Scheduler
- **`cleanupExpiredJobs` must NOT delete `delay_seconds` one-shot jobs.** `delay_seconds` represents relative time ("N seconds from creation"). If the job expired during downtime, deleting it silently drops the user's scheduled reminder. Keep expired `delay_seconds` jobs so `checkAndFire` triggers them on the first tick. Only `at`-based one-shot jobs (absolute time point) should be removed when expired — the scheduled moment has passed.
- **`every_seconds` next_run must be based on `job.NextRun`, NOT `now`.** The old code used `nextRun = now.Add(interval)`, where `now` is the actual fire time (delayed by 0–5s due to the 5s ticker). Each fire accumulates drift, causing the schedule to slide later and later. Fix: `nextRun = job.NextRun.Add(interval)` anchored to the planned schedule, with a forward-advance loop for missed intervals.
- **Recurring jobs skip missed executions on restart (by design).** `cleanupExpiredJobs` recalculates `next_run` from `now` for recurring jobs. This matches traditional cron semantics — no catch-up execution.

### Startup
- `NewOpenAILLM` loads model list asynchronously. `ListModels()` returns fallback immediately.
- Settings save is synchronous — all local I/O, no network calls.
- **`SaveToFile` uses deep JSON merge to preserve unknown fields.** `json.Unmarshal` silently drops fields not in the Go struct. `SaveToFile` reads the existing disk file first and recursively merges struct JSON into it, so user-added custom fields (or future struct fields added in newer versions) survive load→save cycles. Never bypass `SaveToFile` with raw `json.Marshal` writes to config.json.

### CLI / BubbleTea
- **`CLIChannelConfig` is ALWAYS passed by pointer, NEVER by value.** `NewCLIChannel(cfg *CLIChannelConfig, ...)` stores the pointer; all later modifications (callbacks like `BindChatFn`, `GetActiveProgressFn`) are immediately visible to the channel. Passing by value (the old code) silently discards any wiring done after construction, causing nil callbacks at runtime. Tests must always initialize `config: &CLIChannelConfig{}`.
- **`BindChatFn` must be set before `NewCLIChannel`.** The wiring order matters because the channel stores the pointer. All other callbacks (GetActiveProgressFn, GetTodosFn, etc.) follow the same rule — set on `cliCfg` before passing to `NewCLIChannel`.
- **`parseKeyInput` with modifiers must NOT set `Text` field.** `Key.String()` returns `Text` if non-empty (ultraviolet `key.go:392`), bypassing `Keystroke()`. `{Code:'c', Text:"c", Mod:ModCtrl}.String()` → `"c"` not `"ctrl+c"`, breaking cancel.
- **Iteration snapshot deduplication**: PhaseDone + handleAgentMessage can both snapshot the same iteration. Always dedup by iteration number, preferring PhaseDone (has reasoning from server).
- **`ElapsedWall` must be set in ALL snapshot paths** — missing it causes total time to fall back to summing only last iteration's tool.Elapsed.
- **Remote TUI progress: WS now sends `protocol.ProgressEvent` directly** (no conversion). Adding new fields to ProgressEvent is sufficient — no separate WsProgressPayload or conversion functions exist anymore.
- **Cancel key must be `channel:chatID` only (no senderID).** Background task notifications inject messages with senderID="user", while CLI users have different senderIDs. Including senderID in the cancel key makes cancel impossible for bg-task-triggered turns.
- **Cancel interception must NOT send premature cancel ack.** The `/cancel` handler in the agent loop only sends the cancel signal to `cancelCh`. The cancel ack (with `cancelled=true` metadata) is sent exclusively by `chatProcessLoop`'s `wasCancelled` path AFTER Run returns. Sending it in the interception caused TUI to enter idle while the Run was still active — the cancel signal is asynchronous (consumed by `cancelListener` goroutine), so there was a window where TUI was idle but the agent was still working.
- **Cancel records pending bg notifications for the cancelled session.** `handleCancelledRun` takes same-session notifications still pending in `bgRunPending` and records them into the interrupted turn as synthetic tool observations; it also records a `user_cancelled` synthetic tool. Both the synthetic assistant and tool messages are marked `DisplayOnly=true` — they are persisted to DB for UI display but excluded from LLM context (`GetAllMessages` filters `display_only=0`). This ensures the next model call does NOT see the cancel marker, which is correct for graceful shutdown resume (the LLM should continue as if the cancel never happened). The model still sees partial tool calls/results (persisted by `IncrementalPersist` without `DisplayOnly`). Notifications already drained into the Run were already persisted by the normal synthetic-tool injection path, so cancel clears only their `drainedThisRun` tracking metadata. Other-session pending notifications remain in their own session bucket.
- **DrainBgNotifications is cancel-aware.** `engine_run.go` post-iteration drain checks `ctx.Err() == nil` before draining. If ctx is already cancelled (cancel signal arrived during the LLM call), drain is skipped so `handleCancelledRun` can record this session's pending notifications in the interrupted turn before the UI receives the cancel ack.
- **`wireBgNotificationDrain` tracks drained notifications.** Drain copies consumed notifications into `bgSessionState.drainedThisRun` (guarded by `drainedThisRunMu`). On normal/error turn completion, `clearDrainedThisRun()` discards the tracking metadata after notifications were processed. On cancel, `handleCancelledRun` clears the tracking metadata without re-delivering those already-persisted notifications as a fresh user message.
- **`drainAndProcessNotifications` batches all notifications into ONE user message.** All drained notifications are formatted and joined with `\n\n---\n\n` separators, then injected as a single `injectBgUserMessage` call. This avoids spamming the TUI with N separate messages and triggering N separate agent turns when multiple bg tasks complete simultaneously.
- **Turn-ID + `turn_started` event: atomic turn association (replaces InjectUserMessage side-channel).** `chatProcessLoop` generates a per-session monotonic `TurnID` (`bgSessionState.turnIDSeq`) for every dequeued message, stores it in `bgSessionState.activeTurnID` (atomic — `sendMessage` reads it from the Run goroutine), and emits a `turn_started` progress event (`Phase:"turn_started"`, `TurnID`, `TurnStart{Trigger,Content,RequestID}`) BEFORE `processMessage`. The TurnID propagates through `RunConfig.TurnID` → `StructuredProgress.TurnID` → every `ProgressEvent` (via `buildProgressPayload`) + stream callbacks + `emitBuiltinProgress`/`emitBuiltinProgressDone` + the reply `OutboundMsg.TurnID` (stamped by `sendMessage`). `injectBgUserMessage` NO LONGER calls `injectCLIUserMessage` — the notification user message is delivered atomically with the TurnID via the unified progress stream, eliminating the cross-goroutine race between `InjectUserMessage` (caller goroutine) and the turn's reply (`handleOutbound` goroutine). TUI `handleTurnStarted` (`cli_update_progress.go`) intercepts `turn_started` BEFORE the cancel guard; uses `turnStartedProcessed` flag to distinguish "first turn_started for this turn (adopt TurnID)" from "turn_started for N+1 before N's reply (finalize N, start N+1)". `handleAgentMessage` uses `msg.TurnID` (fallback `m.agentTurnID` for SubAgent/legacy) and only clears `streamingMsgIdx` when `turnID == m.agentTurnID` — a late reply for a previous turn must NOT clear the current turn's streaming slot. Web `useProgressStream` handles `turn_started` in the `progress_structured` case → `onInjectUserMessage` callback; the `text` event carries `turn_id` (from `WSMessage.TurnID`). Notification user messages render with a 🔔 badge + muted style (`isNotification` flag on `cliMessage` / `ChatMessage`).
- **`sendMessage` MUST prefer the caller-supplied `metadata["turn_id"]` over `getActiveTurnID` for the FINAL reply.** The progress/stream events are stamped with `RunConfig.TurnID` (authoritative), but `sendMessage` previously stamped the reply with `getActiveTurnID(qualifyChatID(chName,chatID))` which can return 0 (the reply lands on `turn_id=0`). Frontend `commitAssistant(0)` then creates a turn-0 slot while the REAL turn's live shell stays behind — an empty `turn-{N}-live` DOM row above the committed `seq-*` row (user report: "是不是容器写错了" — empty DOM). `handleRunOutput` must pass `sendMeta["turn_id"] = msg.Metadata["turn_id"]`, and `sendMessage` must parse it (fallback `getActiveTurnID` only for tool-initiated mid-turn sends).
- **`bus.OutboundMessage` MUST carry `TurnID`, and `Dispatcher` MUST forward it to `OutboundMsg`.** The final reply goes through the bus path (`sendMessage` → `bus.Outbound` → `Dispatcher` → `channel.Send`). Without `TurnID` on `bus.OutboundMessage`, the top-level `WSMessage.turn_id` is omitempty-omitted (0), and the turn_id only survives in `metadata.turn_id` (the `sendMeta` map). The frontend then reads the missing top-level `turn_id` as 0 → `commitAssistant(0)` + `commitStaleLives` re-commits the same reply as `seq-*-stale` (SAME content rendered twice, duplicate `data-index`). Fix both layers: backend carries `TurnID` end-to-end; frontend `text` handler falls back `textTurnID = msg.turn_id && msg.turn_id > 0 ? msg.turn_id : (Number(msg.metadata?.turn_id ?? '') > 0 ? Number(msg.metadata?.turn_id) : 0)` and passes `commitTurnID = textTurnID > 0 ? textTurnID : undefined` to `onAssistantComplete` (preserve undefined semantics for bang/slash commands). Test: `text event turn_id from metadata.turn_id`.
- **Frontend MUST ignore a DUPLICATE `turn_started` for the ACTIVE turn (`turn_id === store.lastTurnID`, trigger !== 'resume').** A spurious re-emission (SSE reconnect replay of the same turn, a notification turn_started arriving twice, backend re-send) re-runs the commit path: `commitLiveProgressAndReset` (lastIter=0) + `finalizedRef=true` → the finalizedRef guard then DROPS every subsequent progress_structured — the live turn vanishes permanently (user report: "最新 turn 不断消失又出现，好像每个新迭代一次"; debug E2E: iter1 visible → duplicate turn_started → NO-LIVE-ROW through iter2). The stale guard (turn_id < lastTurnID) does NOT cover the === case. AskUser resume legitimately reuses the same TurnID (continuation of the same turn) and is excluded. Fix: `if (p.turn_id === store.lastTurnID && p.turn_start?.trigger !== 'resume') return` in the `turn_started` branch of `useProgressStream`.
- **TurnID is allocated at QUEUE-ADMISSION time (not at dequeue), and REST `POST /api/message` returns it directly.** `agent.chatWorker.admitToMsgCh` allocates the per-session `TurnID` (via `ss.nextTurnID()`, except AskUser answers which are NOT pre-allocated) BEFORE pushing the message onto `msgCh`, writes it to `Metadata["turn_id"]`, and acks the transport with `bus.DeliveryResult{TurnID, Queued}` — the ack is DELAYED from the main loop to the chatWorker (the main loop's `case q <- msg` no longer acks). The web REST response therefore carries `turn_id` + `queued` (web_rest.go handleMessage) so the frontend can bind the optimistic user row without depending on `turn_started` (which may be lost/coalesced in SSE). `queued=true` means the chat was already busy (`len(msgCh) > 0 || ss.busy.Load()`) — the message will be handled after the current turn; the frontend shows a queued marker (cleared by `bindLastUserToTurn` on `turn_started`). **`bus.InboundMessage.DeliveryAck` is `chan bus.DeliveryResult` (NOT `chan error`)**. AskUser answer is a NEW turn: `chatProcessLoop` allocates a fresh `turn_id` via `ss.nextTurnID()` at dequeue time (agent.go:2914-2921) — it does NOT reuse `ss.activeTurnID` (reusing it merged pre/post-answer content into one assistant block). `admitToMsgCh` skips pre-allocation for answers (`ask_user_answered` metadata) so queued messages can't pollute the allocation; the REST ack therefore carries `turn_id=0` and NO `user_echo` is emitted for answers — the frontend binds the answer row via `turn_started`. ✅ **Fixed (AskUser overhaul): the engine's incrementally-persisted intermediate messages now carry the run's TurnID** — `NewPersistenceBridgeWithTurnID(sess, count, cfg.TurnID)` (engine_run.go) stamps `persistMsg.TurnID = b.turnID` in `pendingMessages` (only when 0), and `buildWaitingUserOutbound`'s empty assistant stamps `msg.Metadata["turn_id"]`. Without the stamp, the answer's user row (turn N+1) sits AFTER turn N's intermediate rows in append order, so `deriveTurnIDs` Pass 2 (channel/subscription.go:583) mis-derives turn N's rows as N+1, and `ConvertMessagesToHistoryWithIterations` emits a forged assistant row carrying turn N+1's full iterations BEFORE the answer user row — duplicated/misaligned history rendering. Also: `AppendAskAnswerWithUserMessage` (storage/sqlite/history.go) atomically writes the ask_answer control + answer user row in ONE immediate transaction (crash consistency). CLI/RPC (`send_inbound`) never sets DeliveryAck — unaffected. `enqueueInbound` waits for the ack WITHOUT selecting on `ctx.Done()`: once handed off, the message IS processed — returning context.Canceled would tell the frontend the send failed, triggering a same-ID retry that the dedup layer silently swallows (losing the message).
- **AskUser 协议扩展（web 全面优化）：`AskUserQuestion` 新增 `multi_select`（可多选）/`allow_other`（允许自定义输入）字段（protocol/events.go，`omitempty` 向后兼容，CLI/Feishu 不受影响）。** `tools/ask_user.go` schema 同步声明。前端 `AskUserPanel` 保证：每道题**始终显示自由输入框**（自由文本 > other 输入 > 选项，答案合并优先级）；`multiSelect` 渲染 checkbox 按钮组（多选答案用 `、` 拼接后作为 `Record<string,string>` 单值提交，兼容 `AskUserResponse`）；`allowOther` 自动追加「其他」切换按钮（LLM 只需声明 `allow_other=true`，options 不含 "Other"），选中后展开输入框，"Other" 标记本身不作为答案提交。新增 `components/ui/checkbox.tsx` + `textarea.tsx`（项目此前缺失）。i18n keys: `askUserOther/askUserOtherPlaceholder/askUserMultiHint/askUserFreeInput/askUserFreeInputPlaceholder`。
- **AskUserPanel 输入框 Enter 提交必须加 IME 保护（`!e.nativeEvent.isComposing`）。** 中文/日文输入法组合中按 Enter 选候选词时 `e.key==='Enter'` 为 true 但 `isComposing=true` —— 不加保护会"打字到一半直接提交"（用户报告严重 bug）。三个输入框（other/自由输入/Textarea）的 `onKeyDown` 提交条件都必须带 `!e.nativeEvent.isComposing`。Test: `AskUserPanel.test.tsx` "does NOT submit on Enter while IME is composing"。
- **AskUserPanel 多选按钮内层视觉必须是 `<span>`，绝不能嵌套 `<Checkbox>`（嵌套 button 点击 toggle 两次）。** `components/ui/checkbox.tsx` 渲染 `<button role="checkbox">`，与外层选项 `<button role="checkbox">` 嵌套是无效 HTML——点击图标区域时内层 onClick + 事件冒泡到外层共触发**两次** toggle（选中又立即取消），手机上表现为"点不动/渲染异常"。修复：内层视觉方块改为纯 `<span>`（保留勾选视觉 + `aria-checked` 在外层），点击任意位置只 toggle 一次。Test: `AskUserPanel.test.tsx` "clicking the checkbox icon area toggles exactly once (no nested-button double toggle)"。
- **`useSessionStore` 解析 `ask_user` 事件必须映射 `multi_select`/`allow_other`（后端 snake_case）→ `multiSelect`/`allowOther`（前端 camelCase）。** 后端 `protocol/events.go` 的 JSON tag 是 snake_case；若解析层只提取 `question`/`options`，AskUserPanel 的 `q.multiSelect`/`q.allowOther` 永远 undefined → 多选 checkbox 与 Other 输入框永不触发（"似乎不支持多选"）。Test: `useSessionStore.test.ts` "propagates multi_select / allow_other from the backend snake_case fields"。
- **AskUser cancel（`ask_user_response cancelled:true`）的 `/cancel` 必须带 `ask_user_cancel:"true"` 标记，`interceptCancel` 在"无活跃 Run + 无 pending AskUser"时 no-op（绝不设置 pendingCancel）。** 否则残留 panel 的 cancel（AskUser 已通过其他渠道/回答先到达而清除）落入第三分支 `pendingCancel.Store` → 用户下一条消息的 Run 一启动就被 `registerActiveCancelState` 立即取消（"cancel 掉 AskUser 后，下一条消息被取消了"）。通用 `/cancel`（用户主动输入 / MessageInput stop 按钮）保持原行为（队列中请求仍应被取消）。前端 `AgentPanel` 在 AskUser prompt 活跃时把 MessageInput 的 `onCancel` 改用 `askUser.cancel`，避免误触无标记的通用 cancel。Tests: `agent/pending_ask_user_test.go` `TestAskUserCancelAfterPromptResolvedDoesNotArmPendingCancel` + `TestGenericCancelWithoutActiveRunStillArmsPendingCancel`。
- **AskUser cancel 清除 pending 后必须重置 WaitingUser 的 `ss.busy` 并发射 `session(idle)` —— 否则会话永远卡 busy（"后台 web 会话用 askuser 取消后永远 busy，cancel 无效，什么事情都做不了"）。** WaitingUser 时 `chatProcessLoop` **有意保持 `ss.busy=true`**（防止 chatWorker 在 AskUser panel 显示期间 drain 通知，且不发射 idle）。`interceptCancel` 的 `clearPendingAskUser` 分支只清 pending + 发 ack，**不重置 busy** → 前端 session tree 的 running 状态永远保持 → 后台会话取消 AskUser 后切过去一直 busy、AskUserPanel 已消失（prompt 清除）看起来 cancel 无效。修复（agent.go `interceptCancel` clearPendingAskUser 分支）：`bgSessionStates.Load(cancelKey)` 拿到 ss 后 `ss.busy.Store(false)` + `GlobalWorktreeRegistry.SetBusy(false)` + `emitSessionState(SessionEvent{Action:"idle"})`。Test: `TestAskUserCancelResetsWaitingUserBusy`（红灯→绿灯：cancel 后 `ss.busy.Load()==false`）。
- **`commitLiveProgressAndReset` 必须把 live text fold 进最后迭代的 content（v55 渲染下顶层 content 不显示）。** AskUser WaitingUser 场景：`attachIterationDelta` 只在迭代前进时（`nextIteration > prev`）attach 前一个迭代到前端 IterationHistory，AskUser 迭代后 turn 暂停（无下一次迭代）→ 该迭代 delta 从未到前端 → `iterationHistory` 空，content/reasoning 只存在于 `snap.content`/`snap.reasoning`。旧代码只有 frozen（cancel）分支 fold text，AskUser 提交后迭代 content 空 + 顶层 content 因 `hasIterations=true` 不渲染 → content/reasoning 消失（用户报告严重 bug）。修复：泛化 text fold —— text 非空且（iterationHistory 空 或 最后迭代 content 空）时写入最后迭代 content（顶层 commitText 清空），reasoning 同步保留。Test: `useProgressStream.test.ts` "AskUser WaitingUser: committed iterations KEEP the iteration content/reasoning"。
- **`GetHistoryBefore`/`GetHistory` limit counts raw MESSAGES (rows), NOT user turns.** Counting turns let a single turn with many tool/assistant rows balloon the returned window to millions of tokens (one turn commonly holds 10-50 rows). Bounding by message count keeps the payload proportional to what the client can render. Web reload + loadMore pass `limit: 100`. `has_more` (`total > len(msgs)`) still works unchanged.
- **Compacted-context summary messages render as a collapsed `<details>` placeholder.** `UserMessage` detects `content.trimStart().startsWith("[Compacted context]")`, shows the marker line in a dashed placeholder, and keeps the (potentially huge) summary body hidden behind a native `<details>` toggle — previously the full summary rendered as a normal user message, visually overlapping the messages that followed it. `isCompactMarker`/`latestCompactBoundaryIndex` (rewind eligibility) are unchanged.
- **Turn-ID / Iteration-ID continuity assertions (3-layer diagnostics).** Every layer asserts that TurnID is strictly monotonic (no regression, no gaps) and IterationID advances by exactly +1 within a turn. **Backend**: `chatProcessLoop` checks `turnID` against `bgSessionState.lastTurnID` (regression → `Error` log `TURN_ID_INVARIANT_VIOLATION`; gap → `Warn` log `TURN_ID_GAP`); `runState.beginIteration` checks `i` against `structuredProgress.Iteration` (regression → `Error` `ITER_ID_INVARIANT_VIOLATION`; gap → `Warn` `ITER_ID_GAP`). **TUI**: `handleTurnStarted` checks `ev.TurnID` against `m.lastReceivedTurnID`; `applyProgressSnapshot` checks `snapshot.Iteration` against `progressState.lastIter`. **Web**: `useProgressStream` checks in the `progress_structured` case — `store.lastTurnID` for TurnID (also updated from non-turn_started structured events for SSE reconnect recovery), `store.lastIter` for iteration. All violations log detailed context (prev/next/delta/gap/turnID/chatID/phase) via `log.Error`/`log.Warn` (Go) or `console.error`/`console.warn` (Web). Assertions are **diagnostic, not blocking** — the turn lifecycle proceeds normally; the logs enable debugging of lost/disordered events (e.g. asyncCh full, SSE disconnect gap, progressSlot coalescing).
- **SSE reconnect gap-based reload (NOT on every reconnect).** `useChatMessages` listens for `replay_gap` messages (dispatched by `restoreActiveProgress` in `sseConnection.ts`). SSE event gaps are normal (stateless coalescing, buffer drops) — the existing seq-gap detection in `handleEvent` triggers `restoreActiveProgress` which fetches the active progress snapshot. Only when that snapshot shows a **TurnID change** or **turn ended during gap** (real data loss — committed messages like the reply or notification were lost) does `restoreActiveProgress` dispatch `replay_gap` → `useChatMessages` calls `reload()`. A simple reconnect without data loss does NOT reload. `reload` uses `reconcileHistoryWithLiveRows` to merge DB rows with in-flight live rows. Initial connect does NOT trigger reload.
- **⚠️ 手机设置/后台 gap 后 busy 永久卡死（2026-08-30 "打开设置期间会话完成，退出后卡 busy，最终回复已渲染"）：restoreActiveProgress 的 done/null 分支必须 dispatch agent-idle。** 根因：手机打开设置 → 息屏/切后台 → EventSource 断开；期间 turn 完成 → `session(idle)` 进 ring buffer，但 **resync 场景（buffer 溢出 evict）或断连窗口丢失** → 亮屏重连后 `restoreActiveProgress` 返回 done/null（turn 权威已结束）但** dispatch nothing**（v2 设计——session(idle) 会清 live store 导致渲染消失，replay_gap 只 reload 消息）→ useSessionStore 的 `executingSessionsRef` 残留 busy key → `mergeStatus` 的 `executingKeys.has → 无条件强制 running`（945 行，SSE 权威设计，HTTP idle 也无法纠正）→ **busy 永久卡死**，而回复被 force_reload 恢复渲染（"最终回复也渲染了"）。修复：done/null 分支（turn 结束的权威判定）** dispatch `agent-idle` window 事件**（`window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('agent-idle', {detail:{chatID, channel}}))`）——useSessionStore 的专用 running 清除通道（1319-1337 handler：executingSessionsRef.delete + setStatus('idle')，**不触碰 live store**——与 session(idle) 的本质区别，后者会清 ProgressStore 导致渲染消失）。Test: `sseConnection.test.ts` 的 `dispatches agent-idle when active-progress recovery returns done/null`（修复前红灯：不 dispatch）。
- **⚠️ 侧边栏状态对账三件套（2026-09-01 "侧边栏会话状态经常落后/subagent 卸载了还显示/明明 idle 却显示 busy"根治）：user 级广播 + trust window + sessions-resync + subagent removed 事件 + waiting_input。** 四层修复缺一不可，且互相配套：① **session 事件 user 级广播**——`WebChannel.SendSessionState` 除 route-scoped 投递外，把轻量事件（busy/idle/created/deleted/renamed/subagent_*，`isSidebarSessionEvent` 判定；**history_rewound 除外**——传输屏障语义必须保持 route-scoped）fan-out 给全部 web 客户端（`hub.broadcastSessionStateToWebClients`，`seq=0` 控制面语义、不入 ring buffer、排除事件自身路由订阅者——不重复投递）；侧边栏是 user 级视图，事件只走单路由导致"会话 A 的事件永远到不了正在看 B 的浏览器"。RemoteCLIChannel 对 cli 渠道事件做同样 fan-out（sharedServerHub 模式下 cli 事件不发布到 web channel，它是唯一可达路径）。前端 `handleEvent` 对 `seq=0` 无 id 行消息不过去重 gate（`seq > 0` gate 只对业务消息适用）。② **executingSessionsRef 改 Map<key, ts> + trust window**——busy 事件打时间戳，SSE busy 在 15s（`EXECUTING_TRUST_WINDOW_MS`）内胜过 HTTP idle（chatCancelCh RPC 滞后于 idle 事件的固有竞态），**过期后 HTTP 权威**（丢弃 key）——丢 idle（ring 淘汰/断连/跨路由）不再永久 running。subagent_started 同样打时间戳；mergeStatus 的 agent 无条件 carry 删除（HTTP IsProcessingByChannel 对 agent 查 interactiveSubAgents，已准确）。③ **sessions-resync window 事件**——SSE 断连重连（onopen）、resync_required（buffer 淘汰）、replay_gap(force_reload)（迭代空洞）、restoreActiveProgress done/null（turn 结束）四处触发，useSessionStore 收到后**清全部 executing keys + refresh()**（事件已丢的场合 SSE hints 无意义，HTTP 全量权威）。30s 兜底 poll（仅 busy 时）+ visibilitychange 可见时 reconcile。④ **subagent_stopped removed 事件**——`destroyInteractiveSession` 单漏斗 emit `Removed:true`（TTL 逐出/unload/cancel/panic/spawn 清理统一覆盖；自然完成不走 destroy 所以保持 removed=false idle）；前端 `subagent_stopped(removed=true)` 立即删行（transient + tree），否则销毁的 subagent 在侧边栏残留成"永远 idle 的死行"或 transient 10min TTL 复活。⑤ **waiting_input**——WaitingUser（AskUser 挂起）时 chatCancelCh 已删（IsProcessingByChannel false）但面板显示 busy（快照未清）→ 侧边栏 idle 面板 busy 分裂；`applyWebRunningStatus` 用 `HasPendingAskUserFast`（内存查表，非 DB Replay——session-tree 每行一次 Replay 太贵）输出 `running:true + status:"waiting_input"` 统一状态。⑥ **缓存 strip**——loadSessionTreeCache 恢复时 strip `running/waiting_input` → idle（本地缓存只存结构不存活性——新会话树 RPC 是权威）。测试：Go `web_sse_test.go:TestSSESessionBroadcastSidebarFanoutAndRouteIsolation`（seq=0 fan-out + CLI 排除 + 重复投递排除）+ `agent/interactive_destroy_emit_test.go`（destroy/TTL emit + idempotent + parseInteractiveKeyParent + HasPendingAskUserFast）+ vitest `useSessionStore.test.ts` 'HTTP corrects a stale busy key after the trust window' / 'sessions-resync clears stale executing keys' / 'subagent_stopped removed=true deletes the sidebar row' / 'loadSessionTreeCache strips volatile running' + `sseConnection.test.ts` 'resync_required dispatches sessions-resync'。

- **`restoreActiveProgress` done/null branch MUST reload UNCONDITIONALLY but MUST NOT clear the live store.** `get_active_progress` returning `null`/`phase='done'` means the turn ended on the server (or an active turn momentarily unregistered). The DB is authoritative — reload from it. Two rules: (1) the reload must run BEFORE the progressVersion check (any event during the reconnect window bumps progressVersion and the old code returned early, skipping the reload — the turn vanished); (2) the branch must NOT dispatch `phase='done'`/`session(idle)` — `liveMessage` returns null on `phase='done'`, so the ENTIRE live turn vanishes and recovery depends on the reload (slow SSE = long vanish; user report "这后面原本有十几个迭代，突然消失，过一会出现"). Keep the live row; the reload's committed message merges via `buildMessageRows`' same-turn merge. Sidebar busy clears via the backend's own `session(idle)`.
- **SSE reconnect must fill iteration gaps via `from_iteration` watermark.** `restoreActiveProgress` derives the watermark from the cached `iteration_history`'s LAST COMPLETED iteration (NOT the in-progress `iteration` field — that would skip a just-completed iteration with the same number), then calls `get_active_progress` with `from_iteration`. Backend `GetActiveProgress(FetchSinceWatermark(n))` returns only `iteration > n` deltas; `progressStore.appendIterations` dedups by iteration number. Without this, an SSE disconnect during which the agent advanced many iterations would render a non-linear history (missing middle iterations). `from_iteration=0` (no cache, first restore) returns all 1-based iterations.
- **Gap-too-large guard: `GetActiveProgress` returns `resync_required` instead of transferring a huge incremental `iterationHistory`.** When an incremental pull (`from_iter >= 0`) would return more than `maxIncrementalIterations` (30) iteration-history entries (long SSE disconnect / reconnect gap where the agent advanced dozens of iterations), the backend sets `ResyncRequired=true` + empties `IterationHistory` — the client is signalled to reload from DB (authoritative) rather than consume a large, error-prone delta. Frontend `restoreActiveProgress` treats `resync_required` like the done/null branch: dispatch `replay_gap` → `useChatMessages` reloads from DB. MUST NOT dispatch `progress_structured` with the resync snapshot (its `iterationHistory` is empty — applying it clears the live store). `FetchAll` (`from_iter=-1`, `/su` switch / initial restore) is exempt and always returns everything. Tests: `TestGetActiveProgress_GapTooLarge_ResyncRequired` (Go) + "reloads from DB when active-progress recovery returns resync_required" (frontend).
- **`isProgressLifecycleEvent` MUST NOT include `stream_content` — it breaks SSE reconnect recovery (漏 iter).** `restoreActiveProgress`'s staleness check compares `progressVersion` captured BEFORE the async `get_active_progress` RPC against the value at RPC return. During an SSE reconnect the agent is usually STILL STREAMING — `stream_content` arrives at ~20/sec, each bumping `progressVersion`, so the RPC's `progressVersion !== progressVersion` check ALWAYS fails → recovery is dropped → iterations completed during the disconnect are permanently lost. `stream_content` is a pure stream delta (text append), NOT a lifecycle change. Only `progress_structured` / `sync_progress` / `text` / `session(busy|idle|deleted|HistoryCompacted)` count as lifecycle events.
