- **cancel 后发新 user msg，被 cancel 的 turn 的 live progress 在 user msg 后重复渲染 —— commitLiveProgressAndReset 的 turnID 必须用 MessageStore 实际 live 的 turnID 对齐，不能用可能过时的 `store.lastTurnID`。** 根因：`ProgressStore.lastTurnID` 在 turn_started 于 SSE 上丢失时停留在旧值（N-1），而 `MessageStore` 的 live（含 frozen cancel live）由事件 `turn_id` 写入正确 slot N。旧代码 `commitLiveProgressAndReset` 用 `store.lastTurnID` 调 `onAssistantComplete` → `commitAssistant(N-1)`，cancel 内容落入 slot N-1；`beginTurn(N+1)` 的 `commitStaleLives` 又因 frozen live 跳过 slot N → `toRows` 同时输出 slot N-1 的 assistant 和 slot N 的 `turn-N-live` → 重复渲染（用户报告："cancel 一个消息后发新 user msg，被 cancel 的 turn 的 live progress 在 user msg 后重复渲染，疑似少了 turn id"）。修复：`commitLiveProgressAndReset(store, complete, messageStore)` 用 `messageStore.liveTurnIDWithContent()`（优先返回 frozen live 的 turnID，空 live 壳不参与）覆盖 `store.lastTurnID`。Tests: `liveTurnIDWithContent：优先返回 frozen live 的 turnID` + `cancel 后发新消息：commit 到 frozen live 的 turnID → 合并渲染不重复`。
- **cancel 后发新消息 100% 触发 thinking consistency check（思考中下方出现 turnID=null 的 committed assistant seq-600180）—— 两个根因都在 useProgressStream 的 text 分支。** (1) **cancel 的 turn 的迟到 text（turn_id 缺失）→ `commitTurnID=undefined` → `commitAssistant(0)` 产生 turnID=0 幽灵行**（id=`seq-${eventSeq}`，如 seq-600180 带 88 迭代）：cancel 后 ProgressStore 保持 `phase='frozen'` 直到新 turn 的第一个 structured 事件，此窗口内的 turn_id=0 text 是 cancel turn 的迟到 reply。修复：text 分支开头 `if (textTurnID === 0 && store.getSnapshot().phase === 'frozen') return`（丢弃迟到 reply）；非 frozen 时 `effTextTurnID = store.lastTurnID` fallback（避免 commit 到 0）。(2) **cancel 时 MessageStore 的 live 未 freeze**（writeLiveToMessageStore 不在 text(cancelled) 分支调用）→ 下一个 beginTurn 的 `commitStaleLives` 把它 commit 成 `seq-*-stale`，覆盖 commitLiveProgressAndReset 已 commit 的 assistant。修复：text(cancelled) 分支加 `messageStore.liveTurnIDWithContent()` → `messageStore.freeze(liveTurn)`（与 ProgressStore freeze 同步）。Tests: `cancel 后迟到 text（turn_id 缺失）不 commit 到 turnID=0` + `cancel 时 MessageStore live 被 freeze（commitStaleLives 不产生 seq-*-stale）`。

- **cancel 后发新 user msg，被 cancel 的 turn 的最新 iter 瞬间消失 —— `commitLiveProgressAndReset` 的 frozen 分支必须把进行中 iter 完整 fold 成新条目。** 根因链：进行中 iter k+1 **不在 `iterationHistory`**（只存在于 current 的流式字段 + activeTools/completedTools/streamingTools），且 `freeze()` 的 markError 使 liveTools 折叠条件（`running/generating`）恒空 → iter k+1 的工具/内容无路径进入 committed iterations；`resetProgress` 无条件 `store.reset()` 经 complete 回调清空 frozen 快照。修复：`commitLiveProgressAndReset` frozen 分支把进行中 iter 完整 fold 成**新条目**（`iteration=max(iterationHistory)+1`、content=text、reasoning=liveReasoning、tools=activeTools∪completedTools∪streamingTools **含 error**，`dedupToolsByName` 去重）。Test: `useProgressStream.test.ts` "cancel → new user msg: committed iterations KEEP the frozen in-flight iteration"。
- **⚠️ 撤销 resetProgress 的 frozen 检查 —— frozen 快照在 commit 后必须 reset（否则残留到下一 turn → 重复渲染）。** 曾给 `resetProgress` 加 `if (phase !== 'frozen') store.reset()`（想"保留 frozen 快照"），但副作用：turn N cancel（frozen）→ 用户发消息 → turn_started(N+1) 的 commitLiveProgressAndReset fold 完整数据到 committed → complete 回调 → resetProgress **跳过 reset** → turn N 的 frozen 状态（iterationHistory + current）残留 → turn N+1 的 live 渲染旧 turn 迭代（100% 复现：只要发新消息，旧 turn 迭代在 user msg 后重复渲染）。**原则：commitLiveProgressAndReset 的 frozen fold 已经把完整数据写进 committed，frozen 快照不是唯一数据源，保留它只会残留**。resetProgress 必须无条件 `store.reset()`。Test: 同上的 cancel 测试断言"新 turn 的 ProgressStore 干净（phase 非 frozen + iterationHistory 空）"。
- **⚠️ `MessageStore.toRows()` 的 frozen 合并分支必须输出 `isPartial: true`（V5）——否则 cancel 后正在执行的 tool（最新 iter）从 UI 消失。** 根因：`toRows()` 里 `if (slot.live?.frozen)` 分支用 `{...slot.assistant}` 输出合并行，**继承了 assistant 的 `isPartial: false`**；而非 frozen live 分支（`else if slot.live`）有 `isPartial: true, id: turn-${tid}-live`。MessageList 的 `liveId = rows.find(r => r.isPartial)`（MessageList.tsx:178）匹配不到 frozen 合并行 → `liveProgress` 不传给该行 → LiveIteration 不渲染 `activeTools` → **进行中 tool（cancel 时正在执行的工具）消失**（用户报告："cancel 后最新 iter（正在执行 tool）消失"）。修复：frozen 分支加 `isPartial: true`（保留 assistant 的 id，不产生 turn-360-live 第二行）。与 commit-fold 路径（commitLiveProgressAndReset frozen 分支把 in-flight iter fold 进 committed）是**两个独立路径**：commit 路径负责"发新消息后"的数据保全，toRows isPartial 负责"cancel 后实时渲染"的 liveProgress 传递。Test: `messageStore.test.ts` "cancel 后 frozen 合并行必须 isPartial=true（V5：进行中 activeTools 才被 liveProgress 渲染）"。

- **Web message history satisfies LINEARIZABILITY (not just eventual consistency) — see docs/agent/web-linearizability.md.** Core guarantees: (1) `createRoot` auto-batching makes every render one atomic (M, L) snapshot (no intermediate states); (2) turn-boundary commit is one `flushSync` (appendAssistant + store.reset()) — committed message and live-clear land in the same render; (3) `reset()`/`freeze()` synchronously flush P and cancel pending rAF; (4) all ordering (appendAssistant insert rules, buildMessageRows, reconcileHistoryWithLiveRows) is pure and deterministic (backend append-only order + turnID live placement). Stream content is rAF-throttled per frame — each frame's snapshot is internally consistent; turn boundaries never wait on rAF.
- **Web `user_echo` rows carry NO `dbID` — rewind resolves it from a fresh reload.** User messages are rendered from the `user_echo` SSE message emitted at queue-admission time, BEFORE the agent loop persists the row (web_inbound.go documents "the DB id is not yet known"). The echo row has `persisted=true` but no `dbID`; only a history reload (parseHistoryMessages → `dbID: m.id`) carries it. `rewindCandidates` includes echo rows, but `RewindToHistoryID` needs the DB id — `rewindTo` used to fail with "该消息尚未持久化，无法回溯" for EVERY user message sent in the current session (no per-turn reload by design), even though the message IS persisted. Fix: `rewindTo` resolves a missing `dbID` via `resolveUserMessageDBID` (web/src/components/agent/rewind.ts) from the fresh rows returned by `chat.reload()` (which now returns `Promise<ChatMessage[] | null>`); only a genuinely-unpersisted message (queued, not yet processed) falls through to the error toast. Matching: turnID+content (echo carries the authoritative turnID), content-only fallback for attachment-expansion echoes with turnID=0. **TUI rewind had the SAME bug class: `sendMessage` echoes user messages with `historyID=0` (never backfilled after DB persistence without a reload), and `openRewindPanel` skipped `msg.historyID == 0` → the rewind panel was EMPTY for every message sent in the current session (rewind completely unusable).** Fix (channel/cli/cli_panel_rewind.go): `openRewindPanel` includes optimistic messages (`historyID==0`); `applyRewind` resolves the real DB id via `DynamicHistoryLoader` (RPC-backed get_history) with content-only REVERSE scan (`resolveUserHistoryIDFromHistory` — the optimistic row has no turnID, and rewind targets the newest same-content occurrence); resolution failure shows the locale `RewindUnavailable` ("该消息尚未持久化，无法回溯") instead of passing 0 to the server.

- **Rendered iterations must be CONTIGUOUS (linear-consistency guard).** On a weak network an iteration's delta can be dropped before `restoreActiveProgress` backfills it (SSE stateless coalescing / buffer drops). `appendIterations` deduped by iteration number and `assertIterationContinuity` only logged — the UI rendered iteration 3 in place of the missing 2 (1, 3), violating linear consistency. Fix: `continuousIterations(iters)` returns the longest contiguous prefix starting at 1; `TurnBody` renders only that prefix. The hidden tail reappears once recovery backfills the gap — whatever is rendered is always a valid contiguous sequence. **appendIterations SORTS the union by iteration number** (arrival order is NOT reliable: reconnect recovery can deliver old iteration 1 between new 100/101 → [100, 1, 101]). continuousIterations keeps the now-ordered input and truncates at the first gap — rendered iterations are always ordered AND contiguous.

- **buildMessageRows live placement — distinguish by whether live.turnID exists in the committed list.** A live row with turnID>0 and no same-turn committed assistant is placed by: (1) turnID EXISTS in the committed list (frozen cancelled-turn row) → insert after that turn's last message (ABOVE the newest user — no flicker); (2) turnID NOT in the list (the CURRENT turn's reply — its user was just sent and is still unbound, turnID=0) → append at the END, BELOW the new user. Falling through to the turnID scan skipped the unbound user and rendered the reply ABOVE the user ("reply above my user msg" — linear-consistency violation).

- **turn_id / iteration invariants — every field is non-zero (except legacy history).** (1) `POST /api/message`: non-queued user messages MUST return non-zero `turn_id` (fail-fast 500 if 0); QUEUED messages deliberately OMIT `turn_id` (frontend shows queued marker, binds via `turn_started`); slash commands (`/help`, `/new`) are exempt (no user-message turn semantics; turn_id omitted). (2) `processMessage` eager-save PANICS if a user message reaches persistence without `turn_id` — an unbound user row breaks turn association (replies above the user msg). `admitToMsgCh` allocates turn_id for all queued messages (incl. bg notifications), so the panic can only fire on an upstream bug.

- **`commitLiveProgressAndReset` MUST fold the current iteration's in-flight tools into the committed iterations.** A cancelled turn's tool that was still EXECUTING at cancel time lives in `activeTools/completedTools`, NOT in `iterationHistory` — building the committed iterations from `iterationHistory` alone dropped it, so "user msg1 iter1(cancelled) user msg2" lost iter1 until a reload backfilled it from DB. Fix: before committing, fold `activeTools` ONLY when `iterationHistory` is EMPTY **AND the tools are running/generating (in-flight)**. Done/completed tools are already recorded in the iteration history / progress_history; folding a stale activeTools residue (status=done; PhaseDone does not clear it until the text event) leaks the PREVIOUS turn's tools into the commit (cross-turn leak, Bug 2).

- **LiveIteration must not re-render streamContent that equals the last iteration's text.** After a session switch, the hydrated snapshot can carry the same final text in BOTH `streamContent` and the last completed iteration's thinking/content — TurnBody renders the iteration text and LiveIteration rendered streamContent again (the hist-N row showed the same markdown twice, a linear-consistency violation). Fix: compute `effectiveStreamContent` — drop `streamContent` when it equals the last iteration's `thinking`/`content` (already rendered); content that differs still renders normally.
- **LiveIteration MUST render the EXISTING "思考中…" (ShimmerThinking) at NON-FIRST iteration boundaries — NOT a second indicator.** `LiveIteration` returns null when the current iteration has no content. After an iteration boundary (previous iteration finished, tools preserved as `done`) the NEW iteration's area was blank until its first delta arrived — AND the busy placeholder (MessageList's ShimmerThinking, shown when `liveMessage` is null) was ALSO suppressed because the `lastIter > 0` guard keeps `liveMessage` non-null. Result: NO thinking indicator at the boundary (user: "之前那个思考中有些情况没显示"; a first attempt that ADDED a custom placeholder was rejected — "搞两个思考中"). Fix: when nothing is visible but `progress.streaming` && `progress.lastIter >= 1` && **`progress.iterationHistory.length > 0`** (a REAL predecessor iteration finished — the FIRST iteration is special, its window is covered by the busy placeholder), LiveIteration renders the SAME `ShimmerThinking` component — mutually exclusive with the busy placeholder (live row exists here, doesn't there), so exactly ONE thinking indicator in every state (user: "第一个 iter 是特殊的...在第一个 sse 到达前会渲染两个思考中…"). Tests: `LiveIteration.test.tsx` "shows the EXISTING thinking placeholder at a NON-first iteration boundary" + "returns null for the FIRST iteration".

- **loadMore (scroll-to-top pagination) MUST scroll-anchor the viewport via ΔscrollTop==ΔtotalSize, NOT anchor-row scrollToIndex.** Prepending older rows grows scrollHeight; without anchoring the viewport flashes to the top then jumps back. The old anchor-row approach (record first visible row id → `getOffsetForIndex` → `scrollToIndex`) needed double-rAF + retries to wait for ResizeObserver to measure the prepended rows — during that window the viewport flashed to top (user report: "加载历史后瞬间移到最上方再移回最下方"). Correct fix: before `onLoadMore()` snapshot `scrollTop` + `virtualizer.getTotalSize()`; after the prepend, in a **useLayoutEffect** (synchronous, before paint) apply `el.scrollTop = prevScrollTop + (newTotalSize - prevTotalSize)`. The prepend rows are estimated-height at that point; the residual error is corrected by TanStack's `shouldAdjustScrollPositionOnItemSizeChange` (configured to only adjust items FULLY above the viewport). Result: visible content is pixel-stable, older rows appear ABOVE the viewport — "data just got more", never a jump.

- **Iteration order check applies to STRUCTURED events only.** Reason/content stream events carry the backend's CURRENT iteration (`a.getActiveIteration`) — legitimately 1 while reasoning streams before the iteration loop starts, while structured events advance `lastIter` to 2+. Comparing stream iterations against `lastIter` false-alarmed `ITER_ID_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` on every stream event (prev=2 next=1). The backwards+gap check and `lastIter` advance run only for structured events (phase set); stream events (phase undefined) carry no iteration-order semantics.
- **`setStructuredTools` MUST guard iteration regression — phase:undefined stream deltas never roll back the snapshot.** The Web channel forwards stream_content/reasoning as `progress_structured` WITH `iteration` = the backend's CURRENT iteration (`getActiveIteration`), which legitimately LAGS the snapshot (an iter-2 stream delta can land while iter 4 is active). Applying such an event's `activeTools`/`completedTools`/`iteration` rolled the snapshot back to the older iteration, wiping the newest iteration's tools — the "迭代到一半最新 turn 突然消失" report (plus repeated `ITER_ID_INVARIANT_VIOLATION {prev:4, next:2, phase:undefined}`). Guard (progressStore.ts, after the stale-seq branch): when `opts.iteration < current.iteration` (regressed), only append monotonic data (iterationHistory, todos) and return — never overwrite phase/iteration/content/active/completed. Regression test: `progressStore.test.ts` "ignores iteration-regressed stream deltas".
- **`commitLiveProgressAndReset` MUST reset the iteration state on a NORMAL turn change (turn_id changed in progress_structured — session switch / new turn) — otherwise the new turn VANISHES.** The old store.lastIter (e.g. 48) stays, and the new turn's iteration (e.g. 29 < 48) is dropped by the iteration-regression guard — the new turn's live NEVER updates (user report: `ITER_ID_INVARIANT_VIOLATION iteration went backwards {prev:48, next:29, phase:'tool_exec'}` when switching sessions; only user msg visible, refresh restores because the committed message exists in DB). Fix: after committing the old turn's content (appendAssistant → committed; buildMessageRows' hasCommitted skips the live row), call `store.reset()` when `snap.phase !== 'frozen'` — the new turn's iterations restart at 1. **frozen (cancelled) does NOT reset** — already-rendered cancel content stays visible (cancel itself goes through `store.freeze()` in `text(cancelled)`, but a turn_id change on top of a frozen live must preserve it). Earlier "do NOT reset" was WRONG — it only applied to the cancel path, which never calls this function.
- **`useProgressStream` MUST reset the store on chatID change — CROSS-SESSION GLOBAL STATE POLLUTION.** The `store` (ProgressStore) is a `useRef` created ONCE and survives chatID changes (AgentPanel does NOT remount `useProgressStream` on session switch — no key on the hook). The comment said "we just reset it" but NO reset code existed. The OLD session's `lastIter`/`iterationHistory`/`lastTurnID` and the `finalizedRef`/`phaseDoneRef`/`turnCommittedRef` refs poisoned the NEW session's event handling: iterations mis-judged as regressed, late events dropped by the finalized guards, iterationHistory mixed with the old session — the new session's turn vanished (user report: "怀疑和不同 session 之间全局状态污染有关"; only a full page refresh cleared it). Fix: on chatID change, `store.fullReset()` (clears lastTurnID/lastIter/all iteration state so the hydration effect restores the new session cleanly) + reset the three guard refs.
- **`setStructuredTools` iteration-regression guard MUST compare against `lastIter`, NOT `draft.iteration`.** `draft.iteration` is a DISPLAY value that phase:undefined stream deltas can set AHEAD (the backend's CURRENT iteration, e.g. 48 while the structured loop is at 29). Guarding on it falsely regressed the later structured iteration (29 < 48) and dropped it from `iterationHistory` — the committed message lost iterations (user report: committed with iter-range 1-1 after a turn vanish). `lastIter` is the semantic watermark advanced ONLY by structured events (phase set).
- **插件 channel 激活需要 `channels.<name>.enabled=true`（config.json）—— 仅安装插件不够。** `stdioChannelPluginProvider.IsEnabled(cfg)` 对 nil config 返回 `false`（channel_plugin.go:165-170）。插件被 `PluginManager` 激活（`Plugin activated plugin=xbot.genui`）+ channel provider 注册 ≠ channel 实例创建。`registerChannels`（server 启动）遍历 ChannelProviderRegistry 调 `IsEnabled(GetPluginChannelConfig(cfg, name))`——config.json 的 `channels` 里没有 `genui` 条目 → nil → false → **channel 从未创建 → channel_config 从未发送 → channel_tools 从未声明 → display_html 工具不可见**。修复：config.json 加 `"channels": {"genui": {"enabled": "true"}}`，然后重启 server（`supervisorctl restart xbot-server`，注意 supervisorctl 不在 PATH，用 nix store 完整路径 `/nix/store/.../bin/supervisorctl`）。
- **`ChannelToolBridge.Execute` 的 `Detail` 必须只存纯 TSX（ui_code），不能拼接 Summary 前缀。** 前端 committed 渲染（AssistantMessage:209 `code = parseArgs(tool)?.code || tool.detail`）把 `tool.detail` 直接喂给 sucrase——若 detail 带 `"🎨 UI rendered (5929 chars)\nexport default..."` 前缀，编译失败 → **iframe 空白**（用户报告："就算刷新后都不对，里面一片空白"）。
- **`WebChannel.Send` 必须识别 `genui` metadata 并转发为 `MsgTypeGenUI`（"genui"）WS 消息。** `ChannelToolBridge.Execute` 通过 `ctx.SendFunc(ui_code, {genui:"true"})` 推送完整代码（非流式路径）。若 web.go 的 Send 只认 `__FEISHU_CARD__`、默认 `msgType="text"`，ui_code 会作为普通 text 消息发送 → 前端把完整 TSX 当 markdown 渲染成 **code block**（用户报告："一开始 stream 期间看得到 genui，stream 结束后就变成 code block"）。stream 期间正常是因为走 progress 事件 `GenUIContent`（engine_wire 流式提取），不经过 SendFunc——两条路径都要通。
- **cancel ack 设置的 finalized/phaseDone guard 必须带 turn_id 条件 —— 否则新 turn 的 SSE 事件全被拦截（"SSE 在 dev tool 一直更新但前端进度卡死"）。** `text(cancelled)` 无条件设 `finalizedRef=true`/`phaseDoneRef=true`（useProgressStream.ts:1402-1405）。若 turn_started 丢失（SSE gap），新 turn 的 `stream_content` 到达时被 `if (finalizedRef?.current) return` 无条件拦截（在 turn_id 检查**之前**）；`progress_structured` 被 `phaseDoneRef?.current` guard 拦截（只保留 todos 后 return）→ 新 turn 的迭代/工具/内容永不渲染，而 SSE 连接持续到达（dev tool 可见，前端 store 不更新）。修复：① stream_content 分支把 finalized/phaseDone 检查移到 turn_id 检查（`streamTurnID <= finalizedTurnID`）**之后**，且加 `streamTurnID === 0` 条件（turn_id 已知且更大的新 turn 放行）；② progress_structured 的 phaseDoneRef guard 加 `evTurnID === 0` 条件；③ PhaseDone 分支设置 `finalizedTurnIDRef`（turn 结束权威信号），让同 turn 迟到事件被 turn_id 检查精确拦截（busy ghost 不回归——`does NOT restart streaming from a late progress_structured after PhaseDone` 测试守护）。核心原则：**finalized/phaseDone guard 只对"旧 turn（turn_id <= finalizedTurnID）或无归属（turn_id=0）"生效，新 turn（turn_id 更大）必须放行** —— cancel 后新 turn 的事件是合法的新进度，不是旧 turn 的迟到重放。
- **`MessageList.liveId` 必须匹配【最后一个】isPartial 行（最新 live），不能用 `rows.find`（第一个）。** V5 让 cancel 后的 frozen 合并行 `isPartial=true`（messageStore toRows frozen 分支，为了 activeTools 渲染）。cancel 后发新消息时 rows 同时存在旧 frozen 行 + 新 live 行**两个** isPartial 行：`[userN, frozenN(isPartial), userN+1, liveN+1(isPartial)]`。`find` 返回旧的 frozenN → 新 turn 的 streaming liveProgress（含 streaming=true, lastIter>=1, iterationHistory>0）传给 frozenN 行 → 其 LiveIteration 在 **user msg 上方**渲染"思考中…"（用户报告），且 liveN+1 拿不到 liveProgress → 前端看似卡死。修复：`liveId` 从后往前找最后一个 isPartial 行（`for (let i = rows.length-1; i>=0; i--)`）。frozen 行的 activeTools 渲染依赖 liveProgress 是既有设计（V5），但 liveProgress 永远只属于最新 live 行 —— frozen 行不再接收 liveProgress 后其 activeTools 由 toRows 合并的 iterations 覆盖（cancel 已 commit 的内容）。
- **chatID 切换必须重置 `finalizedTurnIDRef` —— 否则新会话所有事件被旧会话 turn_id 拦截（"切换会话后 SSE 一直更新但前端卡死"）。** turn_id 是**每会话独立**计数器（`agent.go ss.turnIDSeq`，`restoreTurnIDSeq` 从 DB 恢复）。`useProgressStream` 的 chatID-change effect（useEffect + useLayoutEffect 的 progressCacheKey 分支）原本只重置 `finalizedRef`/`phaseDoneRef`/`turnCommittedRef`，**漏了 `finalizedTurnIDRef`**。会话 A 的 turn_id 推到 50（PhaseDone 设置 `finalizedTurnIDRef=50`）后切换会话 B（turn_id 从 1 开始），残留 50 让会话 B 的所有事件（`turn_id <= 50`）被 stream_content/progress_structured 分支的 finalizedTurnID 检查无条件丢弃 → **SSE 持续到达（dev tool 可见）但前端 store 永不更新（卡死，思考内容输出一半后停住）**。gap 检测不生效是**果不是因**：事件根本没进 store，迭代号从不前进，`hasIterationGapNow` 永不触发 —— gap 检测是 downstream 兜底，不是上游事件被丢弃后的恢复路径。修复：chatID 切换两处 effect 都 `finalizedTurnIDRef.current = 0`。Test: `切换会话后 finalizedTurnIDRef 必须重置`（会话 A turn_id=50 → 切会话 B → turn_id=1 的 stream_content 必须写入 store）。
- **`finalizedTurnIDRef` 只在 turn_started 提前 commit 内容【完整】时设置 —— 否则迟到的 text 事件（权威 finalizer）被拦截，上一个 turn 最后迭代 content 永久丢失（"发送 user msg 之后 content 消失，刷新后正常"）。** v55 下最终回复 content 只存在于 text 事件的顶层 content + progress_history 里。当 text 在 SSE 上**迟到**（滞后/coalesce）：turn_started(N+1) 到达 → beginTurn/commitLiveProgressAndReset 用**不完整** store 快照提前 commit turn N（text 未到，最后迭代 content 为空）→ 旧代码无条件 `finalizedTurnIDRef.current = store.lastTurnID` → 迟到的 text(turn_id=N) 到达 → `effTextTurnID <= finalizedTurnID` 拦截丢弃 → 权威 content 丢失，直到刷新从 DB 恢复。修复：turn_started 分支设置 finalizedTurnIDRef 前检查 commit 前 store 是否有**实质 content**（`snapBefore.streamContent || snapBefore.content` 非空）—— 有 content（流式回复已到）= commit 完整 → 设 finalizedTurnIDRef 拦截迟到 text（防重复，busy-ghost/duplicate 测试守护）；无 content = commit 不完整 → **不设** finalizedTurnIDRef → 迟到 text 是权威 finalizer 放行（`commitAssistant` 按 turnID 幂等覆盖 slot，不产生第二行）。Test: `发送 user msg 后，上一个 turn 最后迭代 content 不丢失`（turn 1 无 content → turn_started(2) 提前 commit → 迟到 text(1) 带最终 content → committed assistant 最后迭代 content 保留）。
- **PhaseDone 必须把后端附带的 `iteration_history`（最后一个迭代快照）写入 store —— 否则最后 iter 消失再出现（闪烁，iter 产生了就不要消失）。** 后端 `attachIterationDelta` 只在【推进到下一迭代】时记录前一个迭代到 iterationHistory；**最后一个迭代没有"下一迭代"，从不通过普通结构化事件进入 history**。后端在 PhaseDone 时 `recordFinalIteration` 补记并 attach 到 PhaseDone 事件的 `iteration_history`（engine_wire.go:1908-1918）。前端 PhaseDone 分支（useProgressStream.ts:1138）若只调 `store.stopStreaming()` + 处理 todos、**不把 `p.iteration_history` 传给 setStructuredTools** → 最后一个迭代从未进入 `store.iterationHistory` → live 渲染从 UI 消失；text 事件到达 → `completeRef` 用 `parseWebIterations(progress_history)` 重建 → 重新出现 → **闪烁**。修复：PhaseDone 分支把 `p.iteration_history` 传给 `store.setStructuredTools`（写入 iterationHistory）并同步 `writeLiveToMessageStore`（MessageStore live 行，方案 A Step 3，与普通结构化事件末尾一致）。Test: `PhaseDone 必须把后端附带的 iteration_history（最后迭代）写入 store`（单迭代 turn → PhaseDone 携带 iteration_history → live/committed 必须保留最后迭代 content）。

### Web Frontend Rendering
- **工具 fancy 渲染链路（v61）：`IterationToolSnapshot.ToolHints` 是 FileCreate/FileReplace diff 渲染的唯一持久化载体。** Edit 工具把 unified diff 放进 `ToolResult.Metadata["diff"]`，engine 把它包成 ```` ```diff ```` markdown 塞进 `ToolProgress.ToolHints`（engine_run_tools.go，插件 hint 优先）；live SSE 事件天然携带 `tool_hints`，但**历史快照曾丢**——`snapshotCompletedIteration` 必须拷贝 `ToolHints` 进 `IterationToolSnapshot`（json `tool_hints`），否则 committed 迭代的 Edit 只有 summary 文本没有 diff。前端：`normalizeWebTool` 解析 `tool_hints` → `WebToolProgress.toolHints` → `ToolRender.tsx` 的 `extractDiffSource` 剥掉 markdown fence → `DiffView`（GitHub 式红绿行 + hunk badge + 双行号）。其余专用渲染器同文件：Shell（终端卡片 + `[EXIT N]`/`[TIMEOUT]`/bg task 解析）、Read（`parseRead` 剥 `N\t` 行号前缀 → `CodeView` 行号槽 + hljs 按扩展名高亮）、Grep（`## file` 分组 + pattern `<mark>` 高亮）、Glob（dir/base 分色文件列表）、TodoWrite（进度条 + checklist）；默认 `ToolCallBlock` 把 args pretty-print 成 JSON 高亮。解析函数是**与后端工具结果格式的契约**（tools/grep.go `## path` 分组、tools/read.go `N\t` 行号、glob.go `Found N matching file(s):`），后端格式改动须同步 `ToolRender.test.tsx`。Tests: `ToolRender.test.tsx`（27 例）。
- **shell 后台任务提示必须带参数化 task_id（防 `bg:` 前缀污染）。** `BgTaskManager.Start/Adopt` 生成**裸 8-char hex ID**（如 `3f8f492a`，无任何前缀），`task_wait/task_status/task_kill` 按原样查找（**无前缀 trim 容错**）。历史上 shell.go 的 tips 写 `bg:%s`、消息只给裸 id → 模型把 `bg:3f8f492a` 整个传进 task_wait → 查不到。修复：两处提示（timeout 自动转后台 + 普通后台启动）统一 `Use task_wait (task_id=%q)`（对齐 SubAgent 路径 engine_wire.go:1825 既有模式），tips 不再出现 `bg:` 前缀；task_wait.go 文档同步修正（`bg-` 前缀不存在的过时描述）。**任何新生成后台任务的提示文案都必须内联参数化 task_id**。
- **task 系列多 ID 契约（2026-08-30）：`task_wait`/`task_status`/`task_kill` 的 `task_id` schema 必须声明【纯数组】`Type: "array"` + `Items: {type: string}`——不准 union（`["string","array"]` 在严格 provider 上支持参差，裸 `"string"` 又让模型只传单字符串）。** `llm.ToolParam.Type` 是 `any`（单类型 string 字面量继续兼容，union 序列化能力保留但 task 系列不用）；Execute 端 `parseTaskIDs`（task_wait.go）**保留单字符串兼容**（旧 tool-call 历史/宽松 provider 不破），但 schema 是唯一权威——模型一律传数组，单任务传 `["id"]`。`task_read` 保持单 ID（输出可能几万字符，批量读爆上下文）。提示文案（shell.go 两处 tips + engine_wire.go/interactive.go spawn 文案 + subagent.go 描述）统一数组形式 `task_id=["id"]`。显示层（prompt dry-run）用 `TypeDisplay()`（`llm/types.go`），三个 LLM 序列化点（toOpenAITools/toResponsesTools/toAnthropicTools）直接 `map[string]any{"type": p.Type}` 零改动。Tests: `task_multi_id_test.go`（`TestTaskTools_TaskIDSchemaPureArray` 断言纯数组 + multi-ID 容错汇总 + 单 ID 向后兼容）。
- **`filterPanels`（useTabManager.ts）产出的 `grid.root` 必须保持 branch —— dockview `fromJSON` 断言 "root must be of type branch"。** prune 递归**绝不把单子 branch 提升为其子节点**：terminal tab split 成独立 group 后切换会话 → `filterTerminalPanels` 把 terminal group 过滤掉 → root branch 只剩单子 → 历史实现 `children.length===1 → return children[0]` 把 root 提升成 leaf → 持久化进 localStorage → **之后每次切回该会话 fromJSON 必抛异常**（CrashBoundary 稳定崩溃，用户报告）。修复两层：① 删除全部单子提升（branch 只做空子移除；dockview 恢复单子 branch 完全正常）；整树过滤光返回 `null`（不能产出 `{grid:{root:null}}` 非法结构）；② `useLayoutPersistence.loadLayout` 的 `isRestorableLayout` 校验（`grid.root.type==='branch'`）丢弃历史坏数据 → 回退默认布局（用户 localStorage 里已存 leaf-root，不丢弃永远崩）。⚠️ 旧单元测试曾把"单子提升"断言成契约（"single surviving leaf is collapsed up from the branch"）——契约本身是 bug。Tests: `useTabManager.test.ts` + `useLayoutPersistence.test.ts`。
- **radix-ui `Tooltip` 在触屏设备（无 hover 态）tap 无法打开 —— 所有"图标类 hover 详情"（如 `ContextRing`）手机上点不开。** 根因：Tooltip 默认只响应 hover/focus，触屏 tap 不触发 hover，`tabIndex={0}` 也无效。修复（`components/ui/tooltip.tsx`）：保留 `Tooltip/TooltipTrigger/TooltipContent` API 名不变，内部用 `useIsTouch()` 分流——touch 设备渲染 `Popover`（click toggle，`Tooltip` Root 受控 `open` + Context 共享），非 touch 渲染原生 `Tooltip`。这样所有已有 tooltip 调用点零改动适配手机。新增 tooltip 一律走这个封装，不要直接 import `radix-ui` 的 Tooltip。
- **flex 子项省略号（`truncate`）不生效的根因 = 缺 `min-w-0`** —— flex item 默认 `min-width:auto`，长内容把容器撑宽，`truncate` 的 `text-overflow:ellipsis` 永不触发，且子项溢出产生**横向滚动条**（侧边栏会话名过长即此）。修复：`truncate` 的 span 加 `min-w-0`（`flex-1 truncate` → `min-w-0 flex-1 truncate`），并确保父 flex 容器本身无 `min-w-*` 定宽。排查侧边栏横向滚动先查 min-width 溢出而非 ScrollBar。
- **⚠️ radix `ScrollArea` 的 Viewport 内部有 `display:table; min-width:100%` wrapper —— `min-w-0 flex-1 truncate` 链在 table 布局测量中全部失效（2026-08-31 "定时任务气泡超过屏幕范围"根治）。** radix ScrollArea（`components/ui/scroll-area.tsx`）为支持横向滚动，Viewport 内包一层 `style="min-width:100%; display:table"` 的 wrapper——table 布局的 shrink-to-fit 宽度按内容 **min-content** 计，nowrap 长文本（cron message 实测 958 字符含长 URL）的 min-content 把 table 撑到面板外（**实测 320px 面板被撑到 4073px，气泡直接超出屏幕**；Playwright 布局复现脚本验证）。flex item 的 `min-width:0` 只解除 flex 分配时的 automatic minimum，**不压缩 table shrink-to-fit 测量的 intrinsic min-content**。修复：**纯垂直滚动的面板（无横向滚动需求）不用 radix ScrollArea，改用普通 `div.h-full.overflow-y-auto.overflow-x-hidden`**（TasksPanel 已改）。radix ScrollArea 只用于确有横向滚动场景；含 truncate 气泡的垂直列表一律普通 div。**顺带修复**：后端 `CronJob` json tag 是 snake_case（`cron_expr`/`every_seconds`/`delay_seconds`/`one_shot`/`next_run`/`created_at`/`chat_id`），前端 `useTasks` 必须经 `normalizeCronTask`（对齐 `normalizeBgTask` 模式，camelCase 双兼容 `r.cronExpr ?? r.cron_expr`）——曾因直用原始数据导致调度表达式行永远为空 + `1×` 标记丢失。Test: `useTasks.test.ts` "normalizes backend snake_case cron fields"。
- **侧边栏会话列表分页是【后端数据分页】，不是渲染层分页。** 会话多时一次性拉取会让后端 `ListUserChats` 全量查询 + 每会话一次 preview 子查询 O(N)。实现：`storage/sqlite/chat.go ListUserChats(channel, senderID, currentChatID, offset, limit)` 按 `sort_order>0 优先（升序）+ created_at 升序` 稳定排序分页（复现前端 `sortSessions` 去掉 starred 的顺序，starred 是前端 localStorage 态后端不可见）；默认会话（`chat_id==senderID`，不在 user_chats 表）恒返回置顶、不占 offset。`SessionTreeResult` 返回 `has_more` + `next_offset`。前端 `useSessionStore` 用 `hasMore`/`loadMore`：**`refresh` 必须保留已加载窗口（`limit=已加载数`），不能重置到第一页**——否则 SSE 事件（subagent_stopped/created）或 CRUD 触发的频繁 refresh 会让用户滚动 5 页后瞬间跳回顶部；`loadMore` 用 `next_offset` 增量追加，按 sessionKey 递归去重。`SessionList` 滚动到底 sentinel 触发 `onLoadMore`（搜索模式是已加载数据的前端过滤，不触发分页）。preview 子查询随 limit 自然降到 O(limit)，无需单独批量化。
- **插件 `InfoBar` 必须与 Dockview 垂直堆叠（`main` 用 `flex flex-col`），绝不能默认 flex-row 并排。** 根因：`AppShell.tsx` 的 `<main class="relative h-full min-w-0 flex-1">` 是 flex 容器但**默认 `flex-direction: row`**，`InfoBar`（`shrink-0` + `whitespace-nowrap` 内容，宽度=内容宽度）和 `DockviewContainer`（`h-full w-full`=100% 宽度）**并排** → 总宽度 = nowrap 文本宽度 + 100% > 容器 → 主渲染区域被挤出屏幕（git 插件显示 `git:feat/iteration-content-v55 Δ8` 时暴露）。修复：`main` 加 `flex flex-col`（InfoBar stretch 占满整行宽度，内部 `overflow-hidden`+`overflow-x-auto` 滚动长内容）；`DockviewContainer` host 从 `h-full w-full` 改为 `min-h-0 w-full flex-1`（`h-full`=100% 高度会因 InfoBar 占高而溢出，`flex-1 min-h-0` 填充剩余空间）。**InfoBar 在 `main` 的**底部**（DockviewContainer 之后，VSCode 状态栏风格，`border-t`），且**始终渲染**（无插件内容时渲染固定高度 `h-6` 空栏——绝不 `return null`，否则插件出现/消失导致 info bar 突然弹出/消失、布局跳动；用户需求"不要突然跳出来，一直渲染"）。任何新增的 main 级横幅（info bar / footer / 通知条）都必须遵循此模式：**外层 flex-col 堆叠 + 内部 overflow 裁剪 + 固定高度始终渲染，而不是依赖子元素自身收缩**。Test: `AppShell.test.tsx`（main flex-col + dockview host 无 h-full + info bar 在 children[1] 始终渲染）。
- **`store.freeze()` MUST NOT clear `streamingTools` — cancel preserves the latest in-flight iteration.** A generating tool (tool name already detected, args still streaming) lives in `streamingTools`, NOT `activeTools`. Clearing it at freeze (old code: `draft.streamingTools = []`) made the newest UNFINISHED iteration's tool vanish exactly at cancel time — user-visible "cancel 的时候最新 iter 会消失" (fancy memory turn 220 follow-up). Fix: keep `streamingTools`, mark all running/generating/pending tools as error (same as `activeTools`). `LiveIteration` renders them via `dedupTools([...streamingTools, ...currentActive, ...filteredCompleted])`.
- **`streamingTools ∩ activeTools = ∅`（同名工具双渲染根治）—— 状态机/reduce 层必须维护此不变量。** streamingTools 是流式检测中的工具（generating、参数不全、无 label），activeTools 是结构化事件的执行中工具（running、参数全）。同名共存 → 同一工具渲染两个（用户报告 100% 复现："一个有参数 executing 一个没参数 generating"）。旧前端在 mergeProgressState 做名字过滤；新 TDSM 状态机在 reduce 两处维护：iteration case 新 activeTools 到达时清 prev.streamingTools 同名条目（迭代前进时全清），stream case incoming streamingTools 对当前 activeTools 名字过滤。Test: `H: 同一工具不得双渲染`（双向时序）。
- **切换会话后 live turn 只渲染最后一两个 iter —— history merge 必须 union active_progress 快照的完整迭代。** push 协议每事件只携带【新完成】的 0-1 个迭代；切换后 SSE delta 先到（lazy 采纳，仅含切换后迭代）→ fetchHistory 的 active_progress 快照携带完整 iterationHistory，但 merge 只在"无 live"时用快照 → live 胜出时快照被丢弃。修复：merge step 3.5 —— `ev.active` 与保留 live 同 ID 时 `mergeIterations(live.iterations, snap.iterations)` union（I4 只增，同号快照权威；流式字段 live 非空优先）。Test: `I: 切换会话竞态`。
- **GenUI 空白 iframe 顽疾根因 = sandbox 缺 `allow-same-origin`（contentDocument 为 null）。** `sandbox="allow-scripts"`（无 same-origin）下父页面 `iframe.contentDocument` 返回 **null** → doc.write/createRoot 永不执行 → 永远空白（多轮误修：初始化时序/Detail 前缀/SW 缓存都不是主因）。E2E 决定性证据：`docAccess:false`。同仓库生产验证的 SandboxedUI 一直带 `allow-scripts allow-same-origin`。安全模型：组件代码本就在父页面 `new Function` 编译执行（window.parent===window），sandbox 隔离的是**渲染输出**而非脚本权限；genui-escape E2E 守护渲染隔离（内容只在 iframe 内、父文档零污染），不测 window.parent 访问（在父页面执行时恒为自身，无意义）。
- **normalize 多事件：一条 progress_structured 可同时携带结构化+流式载荷。** get_active_progress 合并 streamState 的快照就是 stream_content/genui_content 与 active_tools 并存 —— normalizeEvent 返回【事件数组】`[stream, iteration]`（stream 先应用），非空 phase 携带流式载荷不再被丢弃。Test: `Bug9+多事件`。
- **乐观 user 行必须 user_sent 直通状态机（requestID 贯穿）—— 不能等 echo/history 映射。** M4 切换后渲染源是状态机，但 user_sent 事件若不接线，user 行要等 user_echo（带 turn_id）回来经 history 映射才出现（用户报告："发送出去就应该渲染发送中，过一会才出现"）。接线三件套：① `agentChat.sendUser(content, rid)` → pendingUsers 立即渲染 sending 行；② `useChatMessages.sendMessage` 接受注入的 requestID（REST id = pendingUser.requestID）→ user_echo/turn_started 按 requestID 精确去重/绑定（防双行）；③ `historyToReplaced` 过滤 `turnID===0 && persisted!==true` 的行（旧 hook 乐观副本不进渲染 —— 状态机 pendingUsers 是唯一源）。Test: `J/K`（立即渲染 + 全链路单行收敛）。
Test: `J/K`（立即渲染 + 全链路单行收敛）。
- **`user_echo` reducer 必须幂等（按 requestID）—— 双 user 行 + 双思考中的【真正根因】在 `reduce.ts`，不在 `historyToReplaced`。** 此为"发 user msg 它被渲染两次、思考中也两次"重复复发的完整根治。前三次修复（`2449337a`→`81f65a72`→`7193ff05`）都只改了 `integrate.ts` 的 `historyToReplaced`（过滤 `dbID===undefined` 行），但它们没堵 `reduce.ts` 的 `user_echo` case 这条路径，所以"某些时序"仍复现。**根因**：`user_echo` 是"同一条 user 消息的权威回显"，当时序为 `turn_started(requestID=R)` **先把**乐观 user（`user_sent` 的 pending 行）绑定进 `turns[].user` 后，迟到且同 R 的 `user_echo` 到达时——旧代码因 `t.user !== null` 不入 turn（仅 `t.user===null` 才挂），落入末尾 `pendingUsers: [...filtered, ev.row]` → 同一条 user 在 `turns[].user` 和 `pendingUsers` 各一份 → `deriveRows` 输出两条（`legacy ⊕ turns ⊕ pending`），且每条 user 后各跟一个"思考中"（turn 内 live assistant + 底部 busy placeholder）→ **用户看到 "user msg + 思考中 + user msg + 思考中"**。**修复（reduce.ts `user_echo`）**：requestID 判定——①同 R 已在 `turns[].user` → 零副作用返回（幂等，绝不再生成第二行）；②同 R 已在 `pendingUsers` → 就地用 echo 权威字段替换该行（清 sending、回填 turnHint），不新增；③hint 指向 user 为空的 turn → 挂 user；④全新 → append pending。**原则**：`user_echo`/`user_sent`/`turn_started` 三者 claim 同一条 message 的唯一 key 是 `requestID`，任何"追加而非去重/替换"都会造成双行。Test: `REPRO: turn 已绑定乐观 user 后,user_echo(同 request) 不得再产生 pending 行`（reduce.test.ts）。
- **`user_echo` 的 requestID 字段名不匹配（后端 `id` vs 前端 `request_id`）—— 偶发双 user 行 + 双思考中的完整根因链。** 此为"发 user msg 渲染两次、第二个思考中前面多几个空格（busy placeholder 的 px-3 缩进）"的最终根治。后端 `web_inbound.go` 把 requestID 序列化到 `WSMessage.ID`（`json:"id"`），**不带 `request_id` 字段**；前端 `normalizeUserEcho` 只读 `env.request_id` → **永远 null** → reduce 的幂等检查（①turn.user 匹配 ②pending 替换）全部跳过 → echo 无条件追加进 `pendingUsers` → 同一 user 在 `turns[].user` 和 `pendingUsers` 各一份（双 user 行 + 第二个 busy placeholder 思考中）。**为什么"概率很低"**：`useChatMessages` 的 echo 处理读 `msg.id`（字段正确）→ 更新 MessageStore → 触发 `history_replaced` → step5 按 turnHint 清掉 pending 的 echo 行（兜底收敛单行）；但 **REST ack 先到**（把 MessageStore 行标 persisted）时，该处理走 `existing.persisted → return prev` 跳过 → 兜底不触发 → 状态机 echo 残留 → 偶发双行。修复：`normalizeUserEcho` 读 `env.request_id ?? env.id`（两字段兼容）——requestID 正确解析后幂等在 reduce 层结构生效，不依赖任何兜底时序。教训：**后端 WSMessage.ID（json:"id"）是 user_echo 的 requestID 载体，任何新消费点必须同时兼容 `id`/`request_id`**（useChatMessages 读 `msg.id` 一直是对的，normalize 读错了）。Test: `REPRO: 后端真实字段(id)的 user_echo 经 normalizeEvent` + `REPRO: REST ack 先到竞态`（reduce.test.ts）。
- **`history_replaced` 必须 union 合并（禁止覆盖）—— "发新消息后上一 turn 最后迭代消失"根治。** turn N 完成（状态机 committed 全量迭代）后你发新消息 → REST ack patchUser → `chat.messages` 变化 → 必然触发 `history_replaced`；若 `chat.messages` 含该 turn 的**过时 DB 中间快照**（reload/replay_gap 在 turn 运行中拉过一次、DB 只有中间迭代；或最终行尚未持久化），旧 step1 的 `else` 分支用 incoming **覆盖**状态机 committed → 最后迭代丢失（概率低：只有切换会话/SSE 断线重连过的会话才可能带过时行）。修复：step1 的 committed/frozen-with-output 分支改用 `mergeTurnData` **union 合并**（迭代 append-only，incoming 同号权威覆盖——DB 是持久化权威但只增不减；content 非空优先——SSE text 是权威 finalizer；user 嫁接）。Test: `REPRO: 发新消息后上一 turn 最后迭代消失`（reduce.test.ts）。
- **⚠️ `history_replaced` step 1 的 "live 胜" 分支必须 union incoming 迭代（2026-08-29 "重启后 turn 的 iter 1..k 全消失"根治——竞态根因）。** 场景：重启 resume 复用 turn n + 迭代号续接（k+1..）后，**切换会话/断线重连**时 SSE 增量事件与 fetchHistory 的到达顺序竞态：**时序 A**（fetchHistory 先到）→ committed（1..k 全量）→ SSE 增量（k+1..）后到 → committed 遮蔽解除升级 live（保留 1..k）✓；**时序 B**（SSE 先到）→ lazy 采纳建立 live（**只含 resume 后的 k+1..**）→ `history_replaced` 后到 → step 1 "live 胜"直接保留 live、**丢弃 incoming committed 的 1..k** → 切换会话"有时能看到迭代有时看不到"（用户报告 plugin manga 会话 turn 61 的 1..87 竞态性消失；DB 铁证 iteration_history 完好——纯前端丢）。修复：live 胜时 union incoming committed/frozen 的 iterations（`mergeIterations(incoming, live)`，同号 **live 权威**——SSE 比 DB 新；与 step 3.5 的 active 快照 union 同原则）。Test: `REPRO: SSE 增量先到（lazy live 只含迭代 4）→ history_replaced 的 committed（1..3）后到 → live 胜时必须 union` + 同号 live 权威测试（reduce.test.ts「重启 resume 切换会话竞态」）。
- **`text_final`(cancel) 必须把 live 的 activeTools/streamingTools（标 error）折进最后迭代—— cancel 后正在执行的工具保留（"已渲染内容永不消失"）。** progress_history 不含从未完成的工具（cancel 时正在执行），不折则丢失（reconnect-bugs/generating-tool E2E 断言）。同理 `derive.ts` frozen 分支也把 activeTools 折进 iterations——`rowsToChatMessages` 不向渲染层传 activeTools（liveProgress 在 frozen 时为空），TurnBody 从 iterations 读工具。另：**cancel 后 committed 行按用户偏好折叠（'all'，FoldedLine unmountOnClose 卸载 children）**—— 与旧架构"cancel 后 isPartial→minimal 展开"不同，E2E 断言需先展开折叠面板再验证内容保留。
- **todos 是会话级状态（turn 结束后存活），不是 turn 级 ephemeral。** ChatState 加 `todos`：iteration/phase_done 事件携带时同步；`historyToReplaced` 从 active_progress 快照提取（**含 phase=done 的快照** —— active 分支排除 done 会让 todos 随之丢失，8 个 todo E2E 的共同根因）；`liveProgressFromState` 统一读会话级（activeTurn=null 时也返回 todos）。E2E 语义："todos survive after turn completes / text event / session switch"。
- **todo_write 后 todos 不显示【真正根因】：turn_id=0 的 progress 事件被前端丢弃。** 后端 `buildMainRunConfig` 从 `msg.Metadata["turn_id"]` 解析 TurnID（engine_wire.go:251-256）—— 某些路径 metadata 缺 turn_id → `cfg.TurnID=0` → progress 事件 `turn_id=0`。前端 `normalize` 的 iteration/phase_done 分支 `if (!turn) return null`（`optTurnID` 对 0/null 返回 null）丢弃整个事件 → **todos 丢失**（todos 是会话级状态，不依赖 turn 绑定）。修复：iteration/phase_done 事件的 `turnID` 类型改为 `TurnID | null`，turn_id=0 缺失时回退 `activeTurn`（与 stream 事件一致，`const target = ev.turnID !== null ? ev.turnID : s.activeTurn`），todos 在所有提前 return 路径都透传（`ev.todos !== undefined ? {...s, todos: ev.todos} : s`）。Test: E2E `REPRO: turn_started + tool_exec progress 带 todos 应显示（turn_id=0）`（todo-sync.spec.ts）+ 后端 `TestRun_TodosCarriedInMidBusyProgress`（验证 TurnID 传播 + mid-busy todos）。
- **todo_write 后 todos 不显示（历史误判，保留防御）：`history_replaced` 不得用快照旧 todos 覆盖实时 `state.todos`。** todo_write 执行 → progress 事件带新 todos → `state.todos` 更新；随后 `history_replaced`（reload/快照）若携带【旧 todos】（active_progress 快照滞后于实时 progress 事件），旧逻辑 `ev.todos.length>0 ? ev.todos : s.todos` 用旧值覆盖 → todo 列表消失/回退。修复：`s.todos.length > 0 ? s.todos : ev.todos`（实时优先，快照仅在 state 为空——切换会话 reset 后——恢复）。
- **`buildToolExecutor` 的 RunConfig 必须设置 `SessionKey`（含 physicalChannel override）—— 否则 TodoWrite 写错 key，web 前端 todo 列表不显示。** web 用户浏览 CLI 会话时（physicalChannel="web" != channel="cli"），`buildMainRunConfig` 把 `cfg.SessionKey` 覆写为 `web:chatID`，但 `buildToolExecutor`（engine_wire.go）新建的 cfg 曾漏设 SessionKey → `buildToolContext` 产出 ToolContext.SessionKey="" → TodoWrite 回退写 `cli:chatID`，而 `refreshStructuredTodos` 读 `web:chatID` → todos 永远写错 key，progress stream 里 Todos 恒空。修复：`buildToolExecutor` 的 cfg 补 `SessionKey: sessionKey`（923 行已算含 override 的 sessionKey）。Test: `TestBuildToolExecutor_SessionKeyPhysicalChannel`（红灯：SessionKey=""，绿灯：web:chat1）。
- **⚠️ todos 的 key 必须统一用 canonical sessionKey（RootSessionKey），禁用 physicalChannel override 的 SessionKey —— 上一条「buildToolExecutor 补 SessionKey(override)」只修了一半。** 完整链路：web 用户浏览 CLI 会话时（physicalChannel="web" != channel="cli"），`cfg.SessionKey` 被 override 成 `web:chatID`，而 `GetActiveProgress` 恢复路径读 `ch:chatID`（ch=messageChannel=origin channel=`cli:chatID`）→ 两者不一致。turn 活跃时 SSE 实时推送 todos（TodoWrite 写 web:chatID，refreshStructuredTodos 读 web:chatID，一致）→ 手机端（早打开）实时显示；但 turn 结束后（后打开的电脑端 / 刷新）走 GetActiveProgress 恢复，读 cli:chatID 读不到 todos（写在 web:chatID）→ **todo 列表消失**（"手机端正确、电脑端不对"）。修复：todos 统一用 canonical key —— `TodoManager.sessionKey` 主 Agent 优先 `ctx.RootSessionKey`（SubAgent 仍用 `SessionKey`=subAgentID 隔离）；`runState` 新增 `todoKey()`（主 Agent 用 `cfg.RootSessionKey`，SubAgent 用 `sessionKey`），替换 refreshStructuredTodos / cleanupTodos / postToolProcessing 的 `s.sessionKey`。Test: `TestTodoManager_SessionKey_RootSessionKeyPriority` + `TestRunState_TodoKey_PhysicalChannelOverride`。
- **⚠️ 同一原则的第三实例：`buildToolContext` 的 `BgSessionKey` 也必须用 canonical key —— 否则后台任务通知创建重复 tenant（"会话变两个 + cancel 后 busy + 历史丢失"）。** `buildToolExecutor` 补 `SessionKey(override)` 后，`engine.go` buildToolContext 的 `tc.BgSessionKey = cfg.SessionKey`（= `web:/path:Agent-xxx`）→ Shell 后台任务注册到 `(web, cli路径)` → 任务完成通知注入时 `GetOrCreateSession("web", chatID)` 创建重复 tenant，通知 turn 跑在新 tenant 里（DB 证据：tenant 148721 首条消息即 `[System Notification] Background task ... completed`；用户观察"别的会话错误调用了 sendmsg"实为通知注入）。新 tenant 无 user_chats 条目但同名同 chat_id，前端显示两条一模一样的会话；cancel 目标混乱 → busy 卡住；打开的是新 tenant → 历史只剩通知 turn。修复：主 Agent 用 `cfg.RootSessionKey`（SubAgent 用 SessionKey 隔离），与 `todoKey()` 同原则。**原则：所有会话级 key（todos、后台任务归属、消息路由）一律用 canonical RootSessionKey；physicalChannel override 的 SessionKey 只用于 channel-scoped tool 解析。** Test: `TestBuildToolContext_BgSessionKeyCanonical`（红灯：web:chat1，绿灯：cli:chat1 + SubAgent 隔离）。
- **⚠️ web 端「Reconnecting…」卡死不重连的根因：watchdog 只在 onopen 启动，首次 connect() 失败时（onopen 从未触发）无任何主动重连兜底。** 链路：首次 connect() 失败 → onerror → setConnected(false)（显示 reconnecting）+ startPolling；EventSource 原生重连卡住（后台 tab / 浏览器反复失败后放弃），readyState 停在 CONNECTING(0) 永不回 CLOSED(2)；REST 轮询 pollSessionStatus 的 `readyState === 2` 判断永不成立（EventSource 不会自 close）→ 无任何主动重连，UI 永远停在 Reconnecting。修复：onerror 里也 `startWatchdog()` —— watchdog 每 15s check，`lastActivityAt=0`（从未 open）时第一次 check 即判 stale 并强制 connect()，直到 onopen 成功（onopen 会 clearWatchdog + 重启）。注意：**不要改成 poll 里 `readyState !== 1` 就重连**（会打断正常 EventSource 原生重连，测试 `polls session status...` / `uses the subscribed CLI channel...` 失败）。Test: `arms the watchdog on first-connect failure so a stalled native retry still reconnects`（sseConnection.test.ts）。
- **`text_final` 必须把最终回复（顶层 content）无条件合并进最后迭代 content（v55 语义）—— 否则回复文本丢失。** v55 渲染层 `hasIterations=true` 时不渲染顶层 content（AssistantMessage `finalContent = !emptyResponse && !hasIterations && !liveHasContent ? message.content : ''`）—— 最终回复必须存在于迭代内。`reduce.ts` 的 `text_final` 用 `commitViaText(finalText, iterations)` 把 finalText 存进 `payload.content` 但**没写进最后迭代** → 'all' 折叠的 `lastText = finalContent || lastIteration?.content || lastIteration?.reasoning` 取最后迭代 reasoning（如 notification 的 'Searching'）而非最终回复 → **回复消失**（notification turn 用户报告："Done processing notification" 不显示）。修复：`text_final` 无条件 `iterations.map((it,i) => i===lastIdx ? {...it, content: finalText} : it)`。**不能用 `content === ''` 条件** —— `normalizeWebIteration` 把 `thinking` fallback 到 content（`content: r.content 非空 ? r.content : r.thinking`），notification 最后迭代 content='Searching'（thinking fallback）非空但非最终回复，条件判断会漏覆盖。Test: `notification turn 最终回复不消失` + 3 个过时断言更新（Bug5/cancel 补齐/REPRO 期望最后迭代 content = finalText）。
- **⚠️ `text_final` 的 finalText 属于【进行中迭代】（`max(live.iter, 已完成迭代最后号)`），AskUser cancel 时若该迭代未完成必须【追加】新迭代，绝不能覆盖上一个已完成迭代 —— "askuser 取消后迭代渲染混乱顺序错乱"根治。** 上面的"无条件覆盖最后迭代"只在进行中迭代**已存在**于 iterations 时正确（正常 turn 完成：`live.iter === 最后迭代号`；或 progressHistory 补齐：后端权威快照领先前端 live，`max` 取到 progressHistory 的最后迭代）。**AskUser cancel 特例**：AskUser 工具在 iter k 中调用 → WaitingUser（`live.iter=k`、iter k 未完成、activeTools 为空 → 不触发 `foldInFlightTools` 追加）→ 用户点 Cancel → `text_final(cancelled, content=null)` → `finalText = live.content`（iter k 文本），旧代码 `iterations.map(最后迭代 → content=finalText)` 把**上一个已完成迭代（k-1）**的 content 替换成 iter k 文本 → 已完成迭代内容被当前迭代文本污染（用户报告：迭代内容顺序错乱/文本跑到别的迭代）。修复（reduce.ts）：`const inFlightIter = Math.max(live.iter, 已完成迭代最后号)`；`iterations` 里有 `inFlightIter` → 覆盖它；没有且比最后一个大 → `[...iterations, { iteration: inFlightIter, content: finalText, ... }]` 追加。Test: `reduce.test.ts` "AskUser cancel: finalText 落到当前迭代（live.iter），不覆盖上一个已完成迭代"（红灯→绿灯，iter2 content 保持 'iter2 content'、iter3 追加）。
- **弱网 notification 只显示"思考中"看不到通知本体 —— turn_started 事件的 `turn_start.content` 是通知内容的权威载体。** 后端 `TurnStartInfo{Trigger, Content, RequestID}` 在 turn_started SSE 事件里携带通知全文；但前端 normalize 的 turn_started 分支只提取 `trigger/request_id` **丢弃了 content**，reduce 也不构造 user 行 → 弱网下 `inject_user` WS 消息丢失时，turn_started 是唯一载体 → 状态机只建 live turn（思考中），notification user 行永远不出现（用户报告："只会出现思考中不会出现 system notification"）。修复（三层）：types.ts 的 turn_started 事件加 `content: string | null`；normalize 提取 `turn_start.content`；reduce 的 turn_started 在 pending 绑定失败时，`trigger==='notification' && content 非空` → 用 content 构造通知 user 行（`isNotification: true` → 🔔 badge + muted style，`requestID: null`）。**不会双行**：通知无 REST 请求，迟到 inject_user 走 useChatMessages → history_replaced 过滤（dbID undefined 行不进状态机）。Test: E2E `REPRO: 弱网 notification`（notification-flicker.spec.ts，先红后绿）。
- **normalize 对缺 `seq` 的 progress 事件宽容（E2E mock 省略 seq；真实后端总带）。** 旧 `if (!turn || !seq) return null` 丢弃无 seq 事件 → 事件全丢无渲染。修复：seq 可选（`EventSeq | null`），reduce 的 I5 对 null seq 不推进基准（`ev.seq !== null && ev.seq <= s.lastSeq`）。同理 **E2E mock 协议对齐**：`progress_structured` 载荷必须嵌套 `progress: {}`（真实后端协议；平铺是旧架构）；stream 前必须有 `turn_started`（stream case 依赖 activeTurn）；工具/迭代号 1-based（`streaming_tools`/`active_tools`/`iteration_history` 的 iteration 从 1 起——0 会被 LiveIteration 的 `t.iteration > maxCompletedIter` 过滤丢弃）。
- **发送成功必须立即清 sending（REST ack 直通）—— 不能等 SSE echo。** sending 若只在 user_echo/turn_started（SSE）到达时清除，REST 几百 ms 完成却没人清 → 弱网/慢 echo 时"发送中"长时间挂住（用户报告："已经成功了还显示发送中"）。接线：`useChatMessages.onSendSuccess` 携带 `{requestID, turnID, queued}`（REST 响应）→ AgentPanel 调 `agentChat.ackUser(rid, turnID, queued)` dispatch `user_ack`（清 sending + **显式赋值 queued**（resp.queued===true 才排队）+ turnHint 补填）；`onSendFail(requestID)` → `failUser` 移除乐观行（对齐旧 removeById）。user_ack 的 dbID=0 视为未知不覆盖（user 行 DB id 由 agent loop 持久化后才有）。Test: `J/L`。
- **`buildMessageRows` turnID=0 dedup must match CONTENT or ITERATIONS, not "any committed assistant".** The old `hasAnyCommittedAssistant` check (0ac17e66, cancel duplicate fix) skipped ANY turnID=0 live row whenever history contained a committed assistant — hiding legitimate streaming when `turn_started` was absent/lost (regression: stream-jitter E2E 'Starting...' never rendered with 100 history messages). Fix: only skip when a committed assistant's content === live content OR its iteration numbers exactly match the live iterations (cancel commit path commits the same text/progress_history). Empty `iterations: []` arrays are truthy — guard with `length > 0` or `0 === 0` falsely matches. Regression tests in `MessageList.test.tsx` (content match skip, iteration match skip, different-content render, different-iteration render).
- **`resetProgress` MUST `store.reset()` on NORMAL completion — the final-iteration duplicate fix.** cf81be66 made it a no-op (to avoid freeze()-induced flicker: phase='frozen' made liveMessage return null). That guard is obsolete since f4c43a45 removed the frozen null-check — `reset()` inside `flushSync` (AgentPanel `onAssistantComplete`: appendAssistant + resetProgress) renders committed + cleared-live ATOMICALLY (no flicker). A no-op left the live row rendering after commit, and when the committed message's turnID didn't match the live turnID, the SAME final reply rendered twice (live `turn-1311-live` + committed `seq-80810`, user report "最终 iter 重复渲染"). **Cancel does NOT call resetProgress** — `text(cancelled)` uses `store.freeze()` (keeps frozen live visible), so the 'already-rendered content never disappears' guarantee is preserved. `buildMessageRows` also got a content-match fallback: the text event can arrive WITHOUT `turn_id` (backend gap / restart recovery) → appendAssistant commits with turnID=0 while the live row keeps its real turnID → exact turnID `hasCommitted` fails → both rows render. Fall back to exact content match (same non-empty content = same message). Tests in `MessageList.test.tsx` (committed turnID=0 + same content → live deduped; different content → live kept).
- **User messages MUST be persisted with their turn_id (eager-save).** `processMessage` eager-saves the user row via `AppendMessage` but built `userMsg` with `TurnID=0` — every reloaded user message carried `turn_id=0`, so the frontend could not locate a turn's user by turnID and `insertBeforeLastUser` (late-committed assistant) fell through to the END of the list → turn N's history rendered below turn N+1's user/live (interleaved turns). Fix: stamp `msg.Metadata["turn_id"]` onto the user row at eager-save. `appendMessageWith` already writes `TurnID`.
- **Rendered order = strict (turnID, role) sort in `buildMessageRows` (`web/src/components/agent/messageOrder.ts`) — the single render choke point.** `bindTurnIDs` derives a deterministic turn_id for every `turnID=0` committed row (assistant → nearest preceding turn; **user → nearest FOLLOWING turn — including optimistic rows**: buildMessageRows binds `[messages, live]` together, so an optimistic user whose reply is streaming binds to that turn and sorts user-before-assistant; only when NO following turn exists do persisted echoes fall back to prevTurn and optimistic rows stay 0 → bottom awaiting their own turn_started), `orderMessageRows` stable-sorts by `(turnID, roleRank)` (user 0 < assistant 1), ties keep input order (= DB append order = iteration order). This REPLACES the old "accumulation order, NEVER re-sort" contract: SSE events can arrive out of order on weak networks (turn 5's text before turn 4's), so the render layer must enforce order rather than trust arrival order. **turn_id=0 committed rows no longer render in-place — they bind and sort.** Legacy rows with NO derivable turn (persisted) sort to the top; optimistic/live (persisted=false/isPartial) sort to the bottom. Fast paths: bind/sort/dedup all return the input array as-is (zero copy) when nothing needs binding/sorting/deduping; `MessageList` caches `committedRows` in useMemo so streaming frames only process the live row (one O(N) scan + one copy per frame).
- **`liveMessage.turnID` MUST NOT fall back to `store.lastTurnID` for a STREAMING live — only for the frozen (cancelled) phase.** `liveMessage.turnID = snap.turnID || (snap.phase === 'frozen' ? store.lastTurnID : 0)`. When the current turn's `turn_started` is lost (SSE coalescing/gap), `snap.turnID=0` and the old `snap.turnID || store.lastTurnID` fallback returned the PREVIOUS turn's id — `buildMessageRows`' same-turn merge then absorbed the streaming live into the OLD committed assistant (same turnID) and the turn VANISHED mid-stream with no replacement (user report: "turn 在 streaming 过程中突然消失，显示还是 busy 但只看得到最后一个 user msg"). Streaming live with `turnID=0` sorts to the bottom (it IS the newest content); the frozen fallback keeps a cancelled live inside its own turn. Regression test: `MessageList.test.tsx` "keeps a STREAMING live when turn_started was lost".
- **`buildMessageRows` same-turn merge is UNCONDITIONAL on turnID** — a mis-bound live (turnID falling back to a previous turn) is prevented upstream in `useProgressStream`, not here. Do NOT re-add content/iteration guards to the merge: normal commit-path merges (live = pre-commit phase of the committed row) rely on it, and guard-based merge-away of legit streaming rows was the regression that caused the vanish bug above.
- **`buildMessageRows` exactDup MUST require `m.turnID===0 || live.turnID===0` — the cross-turn iteration-number match is the turn-vanish P0 driver.** ⚠️ **已废弃（方案 A）：`buildMessageRows`/`MessageListProps.liveMessage`/`dedupMessages` 已删除**——live 行由 `MessageStore.toRows()` 输出（isPartial），渲染层零去重（见 `docs/agent/web-message-store.md`）。本条保留为历史教训：**不要重新引入任何基于迭代号匹配的 live/committed 去重启发式**（迭代号每 turn 重置，跨 turn 比较必误杀）。原内容：The exactDup branch (a live row that duplicates a committed row — same content OR same iteration NUMBERS — is dropped) is only for the "same logical message" case: the text event lost its turn_id (committed turnID=0) or turn_started was lost (live turnID=0). It must NEVER match a committed row with a REAL, DIFFERENT turnID: iteration numbers reset every turn, so a previous turn's committed assistant `[1,2]` naturally shares the same iteration numbers as the current turn's live `[1,2]`. The old code compared iteration numbers without checking turnID → the live row was dropped → the current turn rendered ONLY its user message. Real repro (REC dump turn 360): START was fine (live had 1 iteration ≠ turn 359's 2); the turn vanished exactly when the live reached 2 iterations, matching turn 359's committed 2. This is the P0 "turn 消失" recurrence driver: ANY previous turn with the same iteration count kills the current turn's live rendering.
- **`messagesCache` 渲染缓存已删除（"全是缓存的错误"教训）。** 会话切换/reload 时从 messagesCache（消息渲染 LRU）+ progressSnapshotCache 恢复陈旧数据导致 turn 重复、思考中卡死、进度跳变（10 个迭代突然变 1 个）。已删除：`messagesCache`（reload 不再从缓存恢复消息，总是清空 + 网络拉 DB 权威历史）+ `progressSnapshotCache` 的会话切换 todos 恢复（useProgressStream/useSessionStore 改为 fullReset + active_progress hydration）。**保留**（SSE 实时状态，非渲染缓存）：`lastSeqCache`（SSE 去重水印）、`lastIterationCache`（迭代 gap 检测）、`progressGenerationCache`（reload 竞态判断）、`progressSnapshotCache` 仅用于 `sseConnection` 的断线重连恢复（restoreActiveProgress）。原则：**DB 历史是单一权威数据源，渲染层不做缓存**——任何会话切换的"instant render"缓存都会在弱网/rewind 下陈旧，宁可多一次网络 fetch 也不渲染过期数据。
- **live progress 必须与 history 一起渲染 —— historyReady gate（用户多次要求："很多时候 live progress 先于 history 渲染，必须两个数据都拿到后一起渲染"）。** 根因：切换会话/首屏加载时 `store.clear()` + `setLoading(true)`，但 SSE live 事件仍通过 `writeLiveToMessageStore` **立即写入 MessageStore** → rows 出现 `turn-N-live` 行（无 committed history）→ MessageList 渲染只有 live 的列表 → history（fetchHistory）到达后才补全 → 闪烁/错位。修复（三层）：(1) `useChatMessages` 新增 `historyReady` state —— 切换会话（`!sameTarget`）重置 false，fetchHistory 成功/失败后 true（**同会话 reload（resync_required/replay_gap）不重置** —— 否则已渲染 live 消失 1 秒，RENDER_LOSS_ROWS 教训，AgentPanel 注释明确 "NEVER gate live on loading"）；(2) `useProgressStream` 新增 `historyReady` prop（ref 化），`writeLiveToMessageStore` 开头 `if (!historyReady) return` —— ProgressStore 正常更新，只拦 MessageStore 渲染层；history ready 后由 hydration（active_progress，不受 gate）+ 后续 SSE 恢复 live；(3) AgentPanel 传 `historyReady={chat.historyReady}`。渲染时序：`mergeHistory`（committed）→ `setInitialProgress`（hydration 写 live）→ `setHistoryReady(true)` → 一起渲染。Tests: `useProgressStream.test.ts` historyReady gate 三例（false 不写 / true 写 / false→true 恢复）。
- **Notification user messages (`eventSeq=-1`) survive racing reloads.** `injectUserMessage` marks them with `eventSeq=-1`; `reconcileHistoryWithLiveRows` kept the watermark rule (`-1 < last_seq` → drop), so a notification whose DB row was still in flight vanished until refresh. Fix: `eventSeq === -1` rows are kept unless history already covers the same `turnID:role`.
- **`appendAssistant` `insertBeforeLastUser` fallback inserts before the LAST user message (persisted or not).** The turnID-based lookup (own turn's user) is primary; the old fallback searched only for an UNPERSISTED user, which failed once the next turn's user was persisted (REST response arrived before the commit path) — the assistant appended at the END. Inserting before the last user (persisted or not) is always correct: the committed assistant belongs above the newest user that triggered the commit.

- **`appendAssistant` `insertBeforeLastUser` must locate the committed assistant's OWN turn user by turnID, not just the last UNPERSISTED user.** The commit path (turn_started(N+1) / turn_id-change fallback) commits turn N's live content with `insertBeforeLastUser=true`. The old heuristic searched for an unpersisted user; when turn N+1's user was already persisted (REST `POST /api/message` response arrived before the commit ran — `persisted=true` set in sendMessage's `.then`), the search fell through to the END of the list: turn N's iteration history rendered BELOW turn N+1's user/live content (severe turn-order corruption, `data-turn-id=42` row below `data-turn-id=43`). Fix: first scan backwards for `role==='user' && turnID===turnID` and insert after it; fall back to the last unpersisted user only when this turn's user is unbound (turn_started lost). Test: `inserts a committed assistant after ITS OWN turn user even when the next turn user is already persisted`.
- **快速回复（仅 1 个 agent iter）时 turn 结束后 agent msg 消失（最后一条是 user msg）—— `appendAssistant` 的 `if (!content && !iterations.length) return` 太早跳过 commit。** 根因：快速回复时 text 事件 `content=''` + `progress_history='[]'`（回复文本只通过 stream_content 推送）→ `appendAssistant('', [])` 命中空内容 guard → return（不 commit）→ MessageStore 的 live 行（`turn-N-live`）残留 + `store.reset()` 清 ProgressStore → live 行无 liveProgress 渲染 → **agent msg 视觉消失**。`commitAssistant` 内部本有 `content || live?.content` fallback（live 有流式文本），但被 appendAssistant 的 guard 挡住没机会执行。修复（useChatMessages.ts）：guard 改为 `const hasLive = store.getLive(turnID ?? 0) !== undefined; if (!content && !iterations.length && !hasLive) return` —— live 有内容时不跳过，commitAssistant 的 live fallback 保留流式文本。Test: `appendAssistant 空 content + live 有流式内容：commit 保留 live 内容（快速回复不消失）`。注意：useChatMessages 初始化会 `store.clear()`（prevStoreChatIDRef 变化），测试必须在 renderHook 后再设置 live。
- **v55 架构下 text 事件顶层 `content`（最终回复）必须合并进最后迭代 content —— 否则回复文本消失（notification E2E "Done processing notification." 断言失败）。** v55 渲染层 `hasIterations=true` 时不渲染 `message.content`（AssistantMessage.tsx: `finalContent = !emptyResponse && !hasIterations && !liveHasContent ? message.content : ''`）—— 最终回复必须存在于迭代内。后端真实路径 `progress_history` 最后迭代 Content 通常已是最终回复，但旧格式（thinking 字段 fallback 成 content）或迭代 content 为空时，顶层 content 是唯一权威值。修复（useProgressStream.ts text 分支）：`if (finalText && mergedIterations.length > 0) { mergedIterations = mergedIterations.map((it, i) => i === lastIdx ? { ...it, content: finalText } : it) }` —— **无条件**把 finalText 设为最后迭代 content（结构保证：顶层 content 是最终回复的唯一权威值，非字符串比较 hack；不能用 `!last.content` 条件 —— thinking fallback 会让 content 非空但非最终回复）。Tests: `text 事件顶层 content（最终回复）合并进最后迭代 content（v55 架构）`（单元）+ notification-flicker E2E。

- **`commitLiveProgressAndReset` (useProgressStream) — NEVER wipe live content without committing it first.** When a new turn begins but the previous turn's text event (the authoritative finalizer) was lost (SSE coalescing/disconnect), the live content (stream text, tools, iterationHistory) is the ONLY display of the old reply. A bare `store.reset()` makes it vanish in one frame (flicker) AND loses it until a history reload. Both the `turn_started` branch and the turn_id-change fallback (previously 637-line, now extracted) must call `commitLiveProgressAndReset(store, completeRef?.current)` — it hands the content to `appendAssistant` (insertBeforeLastUser=true) BEFORE resetting, so the message stays visible at the same position, just re-parented from the live stream to the committed list. No-op (plain reset) when nothing is visible. A store with visible content is by definition un-finalized — the text event resets it on arrival — so the commit is unconditional (the old `!finalizedRef?.current || phase==='frozen'` guard was replaced; those states are subsets of "store has content"). Test: `turn_started-lost fallback COMMITS old live content instead of wiping it`.
- **`commitLiveProgressAndReset` MUST commit the old turn's live to `store.lastTurnID` (the OLD turn id), NEVER a `snap.turnID || newTurnID || store.lastTurnID` fallback chain.** `snap.turnID` (the snapshot's `draft.turnID`) is 0 when the old turn had only stream content and no `progress_structured` yet (stream events don't write `draft.turnID`). The old fallback then resolved to `newTurnID` — the NEW turn's id — committing the old turn's live into the NEW turn's slot → the old turn re-renders BELOW the new user msg and the new turn's iterations attach to the duplicated old row (user report: "发新 user msg 后老 turn 重复渲染 + 新 turn iter 渲染在重复 turn 开头"). Fix: `complete?.(text, iters, undefined, store.lastTurnID, true)`; drop the `newTurnID` param entirely. `store.lastTurnID` is still the old turn at both call sites (updated AFTER the commit). Test: `commits old turn live to its OWN turnID (not the new turn) when text event was lost`.
- **`stream_content` branch MUST also check `finalizedTurnIDRef` (drop late events with `turn_id <= finalizedTurnID`), not just `progress_structured`.** turn_started(N+1) resets `finalizedRef=false`, so a late stream_content from the OLD turn (SSE reorder, `turn_id=N`) passes the `finalizedRef`/`phaseDoneRef` guards and re-fills `ProgressStore.streamContent` + `writeLiveToMessageStore` into the OLD slot — resurrecting the finished assistant as a live row BELOW the new turn (user report: "user1 agent1 → 发 user2 → user1 agent1 user2 agent2(processing) agent1"). `progress_structured` already had the `evTurnID <= finalizedTurn` guard; `stream_content` lacked it. Test: `does NOT re-fill the store with a stale turn-1 stream_content after turn_started(2)`.
- **Turn-end must clear `streaming` on ALL authoritative signals, not just text/PhaseDone.** Two `思考中…`-ghost roots: (1) after PhaseDone's `stopStreaming()`, a late non-done `progress_structured` re-set `streaming=true` via `setStructuredTools`'s `streaming = phase !== 'done'` — the `progress_structured` branch lacked the `phaseDoneRef` guard that `stream_content` already had, so PhaseDone-late events must be dropped (todos only), mirroring the stream branch; (2) `session(idle)` is an authoritative turn-end signal but did NOT `stopStreaming()` — a turn that started with `phase='thinking'` (sets `streaming=true`, no visible content yet) and ended abnormally (PhaseDone+text both lost) hit `hasVisibleProgress=false` → no reset → `streaming` stayed true → busy ghost after the input box went idle. Fix: `session(idle)` calls `store.stopStreaming()` on entry (no-op on frozen/PhaseDone). Tests: `does NOT restart streaming from a late progress_structured after PhaseDone` + `stops streaming on session(idle) even when there is no visible progress to reset`.
- **Idle 后空 live 壳残留会渲染"思考中…"在 agent 消息第一行（第三个 ghost 根因）。** 即使 streaming=false，MessageStore 的空 live（turn 以 thinking 开始但无产出、PhaseDone/text 都丢失）仍会 toRows() 输出 isPartial assistant 行；`AssistantMessage.showThinkingIndicator` 的 `isStreaming = message.isPartial || hasLiveProgress` 让 isPartial 残留行误判为 thinking phase → 渲染"思考中…"。双层修复：(1) `MessageStore.clearEmptyLives()`（session idle 时清除无内容、非 frozen 的 live 壳）；(2) `showThinkingIndicator` 增加 `Boolean(progress?.streaming)` 约束（"思考中"语义上只在真正 streaming 时）。Test: `does NOT render "思考中…" for a stale isPartial live row whose turn is already idle` + `clearEmptyLives 清除空 live 壳`。
- **ShimmerThinking 强一致性诊断：思考中… 下方不能有任何同级的 DOM / turn / iter，出现就 console.error 打印错误和状态（web/src/components/agent/ShimmerThinking.tsx）。** 每次渲染后（无依赖数组 useEffect）检查思考中所在**行容器**的兄弟链（不是 el.nextElementSibling —— ShimmerThinking 被包裹在行容器内，自身兄弟恒为空）：`findThinkingRowContainer` 找最近的 `data-message-id` 祖先（LiveIteration/AssistantMessage 行）或 `data-message-list-content` 直接子元素（busy placeholder）。兄弟是消息行（`data-message-id`/`data-turn-id`/`data-iter-count`）→ `console.error('[THINKING_CONSISTENCY_VIOLATION]...')` + 状态（depth/isTurnOrIter/siblingTurnID/siblingIterCount/siblingText/listContext/stack）；普通同级 DOM（footer/AskUserPanel）→ `console.warn` 一次性（`warnedDomRef` 防刷屏）。用于定位"思考中渲染在了已完成 turn 或新 turn 之前"的 turn 重复 / 思考中残留 bug。Tests: `ShimmerThinking.test.tsx`（无兄弟不打印 / 消息行 error / footer warn 一次 / 空白兄弟跳过）。
- **`MessageStore.updateLive` 的 `iterations` 永不回退（union 合并），hydration 加 `hasLive` guard——"迭代到一半只剩 live iter"根因。** 用户报告：没向上滚动，history 突然只剩 live iter，高度变低触发 load more（load more 是结果不是根因）。根因：`updateLive` 是覆盖语义（`{...prev, ...patch}`），而 reload 的 `active_progress`（hydration）在 turn 运行中到达，快照的 `iteration_history` 滞后或为空（后端 `iterationHistories`/`lastProgressSnapshot` 是内存的，重启后重累积；`fromIter` 增量）→ 覆盖清空 `slot.live.iterations` → 已完成迭代消失 → 行高变矮 → sentinel 进入视口 → 误触发 loadMore。修复两层：(1) `updateLive` 的 iterations 用 `mergeIterations` union（按迭代号，turnID 是 slot key 不跨 turn 污染）；(2) hydration 的 `messageStore.updateLive` 加 `!messageStore.hasLive(live.turnID)` guard——MessageStore 已有该 turn 的 live（SSE 写入的最新状态）时不覆盖，只有切换会话/首屏（无 live）才写入快照。对比：ProgressStore 侧早有 `storeActive` guard 保护，MessageStore 侧缺同等保护（注释明说"不受 guard 影响"是设计缺陷）。Tests: `updateLive 空 iterations 不覆盖已有迭代` + `updateLive 部分 iterations 与已有 union`。
- **手机端视图切换（MobileAppShell）绝不能卸载 AgentPanel——用 display:none 保持挂载。** AgentPanel 的 `MessageStore`/`ProgressStore` 创建在组件 useRef 里（`sharedStoreRef`），条件渲染卸载会随组件销毁 → 切回时重建空 store → 依赖 fetchHistory + hydration 恢复 → API 竞态下迭代可能少（用户报告："切换工具按钮再切回会话按钮，历史有可能少一些迭代"——fetchHistory limit=100 的 batch 边界切分 turn / active_progress 快照滞后）。`MobileAppShell.tsx` 的 `view` 切换：AgentPanel 包在 `style={{display: view==='agent' ? undefined : 'none'}}` 的 div 里**始终挂载**（流式状态持续更新，切回立即显示完整历史）；Terminal/MobileDetail 保持条件渲染（lazy 重成本面板不常驻）。Test: `MobileAppShell` 断言切"工具"后 agent-panel 仍在 DOM。**手机端导航模型（v2 重构）**：无常驻底部导航——agent 视图全屏沉浸，导航收进顶栏（☰ 抽屉 / 非 agent 视图变返回按钮 / 顶栏布局项）；`mobile.bottom_nav` **按需渲染**（仅当用户把布局项移入该 slot 才出现 nav 条），`mobileTools` 默认 slot 改为 `mobile.top_bar`，`mobileAgent` 不再默认注册（id 常量保留，旧 overrides 无害引用）；MobileDetail 纯图标行 → segmented tabs（图标+文字 label，可横滚）。
- **切换会话必须还原 live iter 状态（hydration 从 active_progress 恢复）。** 用户需求：切换会话后正在进行的 turn 的 live（已完成迭代 + 进行中工具）必须可见，否则 SSE 在执行 tool 很久没有新事件时会一直卡着。链路：工具执行开始 → `progress_structured`（Phase: tool_exec, ActiveTools: [Shell]）→ `lastProgressSnapshot.Store`（engine_wire.go:1809，快照保留整个工具执行期，无 SSE 也不丢）→ 切换会话 fetchHistory → `active_progress`（GetActiveProgress 返回快照）→ hydration（`historyProgressToLive` → `ProgressStore.replace` + `MessageStore.updateLive`）→ 恢复已完成迭代 + 进行中工具。测试：`useProgressStream` hydration 从 active_progress 恢复 MessageStore live（iterations + activeTools + phase）。边界：服务器重启后快照内存清空 → active_progress 为空 → 不恢复（turn auto-recovery 后 SSE 重新累积）。
- **Skill 工具结果永不 offload。** `OffloadStore.MaybeOffload` 的白名单（offload_recall/recall_masked）必须包含 `"Skill"` —— SKILL.md 是指令，必须完整注入上下文；offload 成摘要标记会让 LLM 只能看到 `📂 [offload:...]` 而无法遵循 skill。
- **全局 tool/skill 黑名单（`disabled_tools`/`disabled_skills`，config 顶层）。** skill 黑名单在 `SkillStore.SetDisabledSkills` + `GetSkillsCatalog` 过滤（LLM 不可见）；tool 黑名单在 `initStores` 注销内置 tool + `Agent.DisableTools`（server_core 里覆盖 agent.New 之后注册的 DownloadFileTool/WebSearchTool）。黑名单是全局的（所有用户/渠道）。
- **用户 skill 路径校验必须 senderID 感知（`IsKnownSkillPathFor(senderID, path)`，review #315 修复）。** 用户通过面板安装的 skill 位于 `{workDir}/.xbot/users/{senderID}/workspace/skills`；旧 `IsKnownSkillPath(path)` 用 `userSkillsDir("")`（空 senderID）校验永远匹配不到 → `skill_get_content`（查看）/`skill_validate_path`（导出）对用户 skill 返回 "not a known skill"。修复后：RPC handler 从 `rpcAuthID(ctx)` 注入 senderID；**空 senderID 跳过用户目录检查**（不知道 owner 时不得声称用户 skill 为已知，防跨用户读取）。全局/项目 skill 不受影响。
- **TurnID counter restore MUST run synchronously in chatWorker, NOT in chatProcessLoop.** The restore (`restoreTurnIDSeq`) ran inside chatProcessLoop (async processLoop goroutine). A message admitted by `admitToMsgCh` before the restore executed allocated turn_id from the zeroed counter (`turn_id=1`), colliding with the DB-restored max (e.g. 153) → `TURN_ID_GAP (prev=1, new=154)` and cross-turn iteration pollution (new turn's delta picked up the old turn's lastProgressSnapshot). Restore now runs in chatWorker right after `bgSessionStates.Store`, before processLoop starts and before the main loop can admit anything.
- **Stream events carry Iteration; the frontend treats an iteration advance in a stream event as an iteration boundary.** `runState.beginIteration` calls `cfg.OnIterationChange(i)` → agent stores `activeIteration` per session (`bgSessionState.activeIteration`); `buildStreamCallbacks` stamps `Iteration: a.getActiveIteration(progressKey)` on stream_content/reasoning events. The frontend `stream_content` handler clears `content`/`activeTools`/`completedTools` when `p.iteration > store.lastIter` (via `setStructuredTools({iteration})`). Without this, when iteration 2 starts with ONLY reasoning streaming (the structured thinking boundary event may be coalesced/dropped in SSE), the frontend kept iter1's content/tools and LiveIteration rendered `iter2(reason2 + content1 + tool1)`. **⚠️ 新 ChatStore（src/chat/reduce.ts stream case）曾丢失这段逻辑**：stream 事件类型与 normalize 丢弃了后端 stamp 的 `iteration` 字段 → stream case 无法判迭代前进，只做全量替换 → 迭代 N+1 的 stream 到达时（content 尚未产出、先推 reasoning），迭代 N 的旧 content/reasoning 残留到新迭代（"老 content 到新迭代"竞态，用户报告）。已修复（`9fec6f1f`）：stream 事件加 `iteration: IterNum | null`，reduce stream case 用 `ev.iteration > prev.iter` 判前进并清空 content/reasoning/streamingTools。Test: `REPRO: 迭代前进时 stream 事件携带 iteration 清空旧 content`（reduce.test.ts）。

- **Iteration boundary MUST keep already-rendered tools (no vanish window).** `setStructuredTools`'s iteration-boundary clear previously emptied `activeTools`/`completedTools` — but the clearing event is often a phase:undefined stream delta that carries NO `iteration_history`, so the previous iteration's tools vanished until the NEXT structured event appended the history. On slow SSE that empty window lasts SECONDS (user report: "agent turn 消失然后又出现，sse 到的慢会有比较久的错误状态"). Fix: the boundary clear keeps `activeTools`/`completedTools` (marks running/generating/pending as `done` — visually completed, not misleading); the new iteration's structured event replaces them. Combined with the `hasVisibleProgress` `lastIter > 0` guard (live row never nulls), the live turn stays fully visible through the boundary. Debug E2E confirmed: iter1 Shell → iter2 boundary (stream delta) keeps Shell → 2.5s slow-SSE wait keeps Shell → next structured replaces. Regression test: `progressStore.test.ts` "keeps already-rendered tools across the iteration boundary".
- **Busy placeholder must show during loading when rows exist.** `MessageList.tsx`'s busy placeholder condition was `busy && !liveMessage && !loading`. During history reload on a busy session, `visibleLiveMessage` is null (hidden by the `chat.loading` gate in AgentPanel) AND `!loading` suppressed the placeholder. Result: neither the live progress nor "思考中…" showed — the session looked idle even though the backend was executing a tool. Fix: changed to `busy && !liveMessage && !(loading && rows.length === 0)` — the loading spinner (`Loader2`) only handles the empty-state (`rows.length === 0`), so the placeholder shows during loading when there are existing messages.
- **Stale PhaseDone from SSE replay must NOT reset the store.** `setStructuredTools` in `progressStore.ts` checked `phase === 'done'` BEFORE the `eventSeq` stale check. When switching to a busy session: (1) `initialProgress` from the history API hydrates the store (e.g. seq=8, activeTools=[Shell running]), (2) SSE replay delivers a stale PhaseDone (seq=5) from the PREVIOUS turn. The `phase === 'done'` check fired first → `this.reset()` → store cleared → activeTools vanished. The tool never reappeared because the current turn's events (seq=6,7,8) may not be in the SSE buffer. Fix: moved the `eventSeq` stale check BEFORE the `phase === 'done'` check. A stale PhaseDone (seq <= store's eventSeq) only updates todos (PhaseDone may carry TodoWrite updates) and does NOT reset the store. A non-stale PhaseDone (seq > store's eventSeq or no seq) resets as before. Events without `eventSeq` (e.g. `restoreActiveProgress` dispatching `{ phase: 'done' }` without seq) still pass through to the PhaseDone check.
- **LiveIteration must NOT filter activeTools by name+label against iterationHistory.** `LiveIteration.tsx` filtered ALL tools (including `activeTools`) by `completedIterToolKeys` (name+label) to prevent stale tools from completed iterations from rendering. But this also filtered out running tools from the CURRENT iteration that happened to share the same name+label as a tool in a completed iteration (e.g. `Shell` called in both iteration 1 and 2). The running tool disappeared from the UI — "generating会渲染，但executing之后tool就消失". Fix: (1) filter `activeTools` by iteration number (like `completedTools` — `t.iteration > maxCompletedIter`), (2) only apply the `completedIterToolKeys` name+label filter to `completedTools`, NOT to `activeTools` or `streamingTools`. This also fixes duplicate iteration rendering: stale `activeTools` from completed iterations are now filtered by iteration number, preventing them from rendering alongside `iterationHistory`.
- **`historyProgressToLive` must handle the iteration-boundary snapshot.** When `GetActiveProgress` is called after `snapshotCompletedIteration` but before `prepareForIteration`, the snapshot carries the PREVIOUS iteration's `Content` and `CompletedTools` (not yet cleared). The delta for the completed iteration hasn't been recorded in `iterationHistories` yet (it's only recorded when the NEXT iteration's first event arrives via `attachIterationDelta`). `historyProgressToLive` hydrated the store with this stale `content` and `completedTools`, and `LiveIteration` rendered them as if they belong to the current (in-flight) iteration — duplicating content and tools. Fix: in `historyProgressToLive`, when `activeTools` is empty and `completedTools` is non-empty, the snapshot is at the iteration boundary. Clear `content` and `completedTools` from the live snapshot so `LiveIteration` doesn't duplicate them. Do NOT add a synthetic entry to `iterationHistory` — it would be appended at the END of the array (which is the BEGINNING if the store is empty), causing the content to appear BEFORE earlier iterations' tools ("content跑到别的迭代里了"). The delta protocol will deliver the correct data in order when the next iteration starts.
- **Optimistic user messages MUST be bound to their turnID via `bindLastUserToTurn`.** `sendMessage` creates optimistic user rows with `turnID=0`; when `turn_started` arrives with the real turnID, `AgentPanel.onTurnStarted` calls `chat.bindLastUserToTurn(turnID)` to stamp it on the last unpersisted user row. Without this, `appendLiveMessage` can't distinguish "current turn's optimistic user" (live assistant goes AFTER it) from "newer optimistic user" (live assistant goes BEFORE it) — both were turnID=0, so the live assistant was positioned after a newer user msg, breaking turn order (`[u1,a1,u2,u3,live-2]` instead of `[u1,a1,u2,live-2,u3]`).
- **`appendLiveMessage` insertion scan MUST skip turnID=0 messages.** When positioning the live assistant by turnID, skip rows with `turnID=0` (ambiguous optimistic/legacy) and keep scanning backwards — insert after the last bound message with `turnID <= liveMessage.turnID`; if none found, append at the end. The old logic stopped at the first turnID=0 row, mispositioning the live assistant. This pairs with `bindLastUserToTurn` to guarantee linear turn order in `rows`.
- **Streaming LaTeX：未闭合 math 尾部必须在源级截断（与 mermaid 的 StreamingContext 同原则）。** 流式 content 快照可能以 partial 公式结尾：remark-math 只认成对 `$$` 定界符，未闭合的 `$$` 被 math-flow 规则吞到文本尾，partial TeX body（`\begin{aligned}`、`\\`、`&`、`\item`）进 KaTeX（`throwOnError:false`）渲染出原始 marker / 空结构，随流推进每次 re-parse 在消息底部堆积（用户报告"空列表之类的很难看"）。修复（`MarkdownRenderer.tsx` `clipTrailingUnclosedMath`）：streaming 时从最后一个未配对 `$$` 处截断（完整公式正常渲染、partial 尾部不渲染、闭合后自然恢复）；行内未配对 `$` 截到行尾（remark-math 行内公式同行闭合）；fenced/inline code 跳过；**完成态（非流式）不截断**（内容即权威——注意 remark-math 现状会把未闭合 `$$` 到结尾吞进 KaTeX）。运行在 `normalizeMathDelimiters` 之后（`\[` 已归一化为 `$$` 统一计数）。Tests: `MarkdownRenderer.test.tsx` 6 例新增。
- **⚠️ FileReplace 无限循环守卫（`doReplace`）—— new_string 包含 old_string 时替换是 no-op 但永远"成功"。** 两起真实事故：b300-pd-pool-leak-debug turn 2460（模型 new ⊃ old ×6 迭代死循环）+ **turn-13 web 事故（06:32，升级版）**：`'plugin'` → `'plugin' | 'diff'` 首次替换成功后 old 仍在文件 → 模型无视 WARNING 重复调用 → loop breaker 拦截 19 次（prompt tokens 冻结 300880）→ **参数微调版本绕过逐字节签名检查真执行第二次** → 文件损坏为 `'plugin' | 'diff' | 'diff'`。三层修复：① `infiniteLoopGuardMsg`（`tools/edit.go`）：替换后 old 残留时返回 ⛔ CRITICAL + **剩余匹配数**（`strings.Count`）+ "task is DONE" 明确指引（exact 与 fuzzy 路径都走它）；② **loop breaker 死锁保险**（`agent/engine_run.go`）：`runState.loopBreakCount` 连续拦截计数 + `maxLoopBreaks=5` 达标置 `loopFatal` → 主循环 postToolProcessing 后强制终止 Run（防 19 次空转烧 token）；正常执行（签名变化）重置计数；警告文案带拦截次数并引用"首次真实执行的结果"；③ **可观测性**（`llm/openai.go`）：`[LLM] Starting stream request` 日志加 `prompt_chars`（消息字符总量，含 tool args）——跨迭代恒定 = 新迭代消息没进请求（turn-13 排查时缺的直接证据）。Test: `TestDoReplace_NewContainsOld_InfiniteLoopGuard` + `TestLoopIterationAppendIntegrity`（含 loopFatal 终止 + 计数重置断言）。
- **Monaco DiffEditor（原生 diff tab）生命周期与导航。** ① **dispose 竞态**：`@monaco-editor/react` 的 DiffEditor 卸载时 SDK dispose TextModel 早于 DiffEditorWidget reset model → `TextModel got disposed before DiffEditorWidget model got reset`（window.onerror）。修法：`keepCurrentOriginalModel` + `keepCurrentModifiedModel`（model 交给 monaco 全局生命周期）。② **布局持久化剥离 diff 内容**（`useLayoutPersistence.stripDiffContents`）：original/modified 是完整文件内容（数百 KB），写 localStorage 会撞 5MB 上限导致整个布局保存失败；持久化时 delete 这两个字段（tab 骨架保留），恢复后 DiffPanel 的空内容守卫显示"请从 Git 面板重新打开"。③ **diff 导航**：monaco 内置 action `editor.action.diffEditor.nextDiff/prevDiff`（注册在 modified editor 上，`editor.getModifiedEditor().getAction(id)?.run()`）；`MonacoDiffEditor` 自带悬浮按钮组（absolute 右上角 ChevronUp/Down）+ `autoFocusFirstDiff`（onMount 后 250ms 跳第一个 diff，等 diff 计算完成）。④ **主题**（`defineXbotTheme`）：token rules 用 One Dark Pro（dark）/GitHub Light（light）调色板（comment/keyword/string/number/type/function/variable/tag/attribute ~19 条）；diff 配色 GitHub 风格半透明红绿（`diffEditor.insertedTextBackground` 词级 > line 级行级，gutter 同 line，overviewRuler 标记色）；scrollbar 细化（10px 无阴影半透明 slider）；`renderLineHighlight: 'none'`（diff 场景行高亮与红绿块冲突）。
- **Mermaid diagrams must NOT render during streaming — integrate via `StreamingContext`.** `mermaid.render()` is async + CPU-intensive; during streaming the source is incomplete (typewriter-clipped), so rendering would fail repeatedly and waste CPU. The `MarkdownRenderer` passes `streaming` state through a React Context (`StreamingContext`). `CodeBlock` consumes it: when `streaming=true` + `lang==='mermaid'` → shows `MermaidSourceBlock` (plain source, synchronous, cheap); when `streaming=false` → mounts `MermaidDiagram` which renders the SVG exactly once. The transition is seamless — both use the same `CodeBlock` visual style. `MermaidDiagram` lazy-loads the ~1MB mermaid package via dynamic `import('mermaid')` (module-scope singleton + `useSyncExternalStore` for ready state, mirroring the highlight.js pattern). `MarkdownPreview.tsx` (file preview, non-streaming) always renders `MermaidDiagram` directly. Error fallback shows raw source in a styled code block. **Responsive width**: mermaid injects an inline `style="max-width: <px>"` that clamps small diagrams to their natural width — `MermaidDiagram` strips this from the rendered SVG so small diagrams expand to fill the container (`w-full h-auto max-w-none`), while large diagrams still scroll horizontally via `overflow-x-auto`. **Fullscreen view**: a maximize button (`Maximize2` icon) opens a fullscreen overlay covering the entire viewport via `createPortal` (imported from `react-dom`, NOT `react` — React 19 removed `createPortal` from the `react` package; importing from `react` throws "createPortal is not a function"). Click backdrop or Esc to close; body scroll locked while open. **Fullscreen overlay selector must be `[role="dialog"]`, never `.fixed.inset-0`** — the fullscreen overlay is `fixed inset-0 z-[9999]`, but the app shell main container (`AppShell`/`MobileAppShell`) is ALSO `fixed inset-0` (iOS PWA standalone full-bleed guarantee), so a Playwright `page.locator('.fixed.inset-0')` resolves to 2 elements and raises a strict-mode violation. The overlay carries `role="dialog"` `aria-modal="true"` for this exact reason; any E2E targeting the overlay (or any future `fixed inset-0` portal overlay) must use the semantic dialog role, not the broad class.
